Slang-Q is intended as a basis for studying how current models handle sensitive, community-specific language and whether they can provide accurate and reliable information about such forms of identity and linguistic expression.

</details>


### [166] [Preverbal Uninflected and Underived Roots in Mapudungun. Wuno and Its Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04869)
*Andres Chandia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过四世纪（1606年至今）的历时语料分析，重新评估了马普切语（Mapudungun）动词前非屈折词根（如wuno"返回"）的语法地位，提出"韵律-正字法假说"，支持Zúñiga的"激进连接"模型，而非现有文献中的助动词、情态前缀或词缀等分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学者对马普切语动词前非屈折词根的分类存在根本分歧（助动词、情态前缀、前置小品词/复合动词词干），缺乏统一的语法模型。该语言没有前接触时期的书面传统，仅靠早期传教士的转写记录，因此需要厘清这些成分的真实句法地位，并理解正字法实践对语法分析的干扰。

Method: 采用历时语料库分析方法，涵盖1606年至今四个世纪的文本。系统考察多个动词前成分（wuno、kim、shinge、pepi、kupa、kalli）在语料中的分布特征，区分三类表现：稳定的V1复合词、受正字法变化影响的波动型V1、以及真正的小品词。通过发现kim和kupa的V2（第二动词位置）证据来判定其词汇动词身份。

Result: 语料分析揭示了三类不同分布特征：kim和shinge为稳定的V1复合词；pepi和wuno的V1使用率波动与正字法转变相关；kalli是唯一真正的粒子。kim和kupa存在V2（独立动词位置）例证，证明其完整词汇动词地位。据此提出韵律-正字法假说：早期的"可变黏着"实为早期传教士将韵律停顿记为空格，后被说话者重新分析为句法边界的化石产物。

Conclusion: 证据支持Zúñiga的"激进连接"（radical concatenation）作为正确的语法模型。这一发现对研究无前接触书面传统语言的学者具有方法论启示：正字法惯例可能掩盖真实的句法结构，历时语料中的"变异性"未必反映语法本质变化，而可能源自转写实践的演变。

Abstract: This study examines the grammatical status of preverbal uninflected and underived roots in Mapudungun, with particular focus on wuno 'return/re-'. Through a critical review of scholarly classifications--auxiliaries (Smeets, 2008), modal prefixes (Longkon, 2011), and preverbal particles/complex verb stems (Zúñiga, 2006)--we demonstrate the limitations of existing frameworks. A diachronic corpus analysis spanning four centuries (1606-present) reveals that these elements exhibit three distinct profiles: stable V1 compounds (kim, shinge), volatile V1 rates reflecting orthographic shift (pepi, wuno), and a true particle (kalli). The discovery of V2 attestations for kim and kupa confirms their status as full lexical verbs. We propose a prosodic-orthographic hypothesis: apparent "variable binding" results from the fossilization of prosodic pauses transcribed by early missionaries as spaces, a convention later reanalysed by speakers as syntactic boundaries. The evidence supports Zúñiga's radical concatenation as the correct grammatical model, with implications for the study of languages with no pre-contact written tradition.

</details>


### [167] [A-SR: Self-Evolving Agentic LLMs for Symbolic Regression via Hierarchical Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04872)
*Wenxiao Zhao,Dong Liu,Kaiyi Xu,Feng Liu,Zhen Zhao,Fei Ben,Shu Wang,Wenhao Li,Yingnian Wu,Fenghua Ling,Haobo Li,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出A-SR，一个自进化的智能体框架，用于符号回归（从数据中发现闭式方程）。与现有LLM引导方法不同，A-SR将控制单元从表达式编辑转变为角色条件化的证据视图，通过协调协议路由、在线评估者-奖励角色策略和状态路由过程记忆实现公式发现。框架在运行内和跨运行两个时间尺度上自进化，实验证明其在LLM-SRBench基准和真实科学发现任务上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM引导的符号回归方法依赖于统一的提议循环，将异构的搜索失败压缩为标量分数和单一提示，无法有效区分不同类型的搜索失败信息（如不可靠的表达式、低产出、无效表达式等），限制了搜索效率和最终发现的公式质量。

Method: A-SR提出将控制单元从表达式编辑转为角色条件化的证据视图。核心组件包括：(1) 协调协议路由，协调多个代理间的分工；(2) 在线评估者-奖励角色策略，评估者反馈描述可靠性和产出性，更新角色级别的效用；(3) 状态路由的过程记忆，将精英动机、失败轨迹和有效性诊断路由给不同代理。框架在两个时间尺度自进化：运行内适应搜索过程（不更新LLM参数），跨运行将记录轨迹蒸馏为开源LLM的角色条件化提议先验。

Result: 在LLM-SRBench的四个LSR-Synth科学域上，A-SR将Acc@0.01从基线25.79%提升至48.30%（Llama3.1-8B）；A-SR-LoRA将Qwen3-4B的结果从24.58%提升至38.29%。在四个真实世界科学发现任务中，A-SR在8个报告指标中的7个上取得最佳分布内或分布外归一化均方误差。

Conclusion: A-SR通过角色条件化的证据视图和多代理协调机制，有效解决了现有LLM引导符号回归方法中信息压缩问题，显著提升了符号回归的准确性和效率，并展示了将通过搜索经验蒸馏到开源LLM中实现持续自我进化的能力。

Abstract: Symbolic regression aims to discover closed-form equations from data, but existing LLM-guided methods often rely on a unified proposal loop that compresses heterogeneous search failures into a scalar score and a single prompt. We propose A-SR, a self-evolving agentic framework that shifts the control unit from expression edits to role-conditioned evidence views. A-SR coordinates formula discovery through routing among coordination protocols, an online evaluator-reward role policy, and state-routed process memory. During search, evaluator feedback characterizes reliability and productivity, updates role-level utilities, and routes elite motifs, failure traces, and validity diagnostics to different agents. The framework self-evolves at two timescales: within a run, it adapts the search process without updating LLM parameters; across runs, recorded trajectories can be distilled into open-source LLMs as role-conditioned proposal priors. Averaged over the four LSR-Synth scientific domains in LLM-SRBench, A-SR improves Acc@0.01 over baselines from 25.79% to 48.30% with Llama3.1-8B, while A-SR-LoRA improves the corresponding Qwen3-4B result from 24.58% to 38.29%. On four real-world scientific discovery tasks, A-SR obtains the best in-distribution or out-of-distribution normalized mean squared error on 7 of 8 reported metrics.

</details>


### [168] [Evaluation Pitfalls and Sparsity Limitations in LLM-based Confidence Estimates for Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04899)
*Elena Merdjanovska,Omar Zaidan,Andreas Rücklé*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM置信度估计中的"口头化"(verbalization)方法会产生极其稀疏的输出（如Qwen3-32B在SST-2上仅输出8个唯一置信度值，其中超一半恰好为95%），这不仅限制了实际用途，还会严重影响评估结果——AUARC的插值方式选择会显著改变模型排名。作者主张标准化阶梯插值以获得公平比较，并提出"verbalization logprobs"方法（用token概率加权口头化的数字），在不增加推理成本的情况下解决稀疏性问题并取得最佳AUARC（比vanilla verbalization高2.3个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM用于分类时需要置信度估计来判断预测可信度，但常见的verbalization方法产生极度稀疏的置信度输出（如Qwen3-32B在SST-2上仅输出8个唯一值，过半恰好是95%），这既限制了实际应用价值，也使得评估结果不可靠，因为AUARC的插值选择会戏剧性地改变模型排名。

Method: 作者在四个数据集和两个LLM上进行实验，系统性地分析verbalization输出的稀疏性问题，并揭示其对AUARC评估的影响。提出"verbalization logprobs"方法，即通过将口头化输出中数字的token概率进行加权来作为置信度，以缓解稀疏性。同时倡导使用阶梯插值（stepwise interpolation）标准化AUARC计算以确保公平比较。

Result: Qwen3-32B在SST-2上verbalize仅8个唯一置信度值且超半数为95%，该模式在四个数据集和两个LLM上一致出现；AUARC中线性插值与阶梯插值会显著改变排名，一致性采样（consistency sampling）在两种插值下从最好变为最差；verbalization logprobs方法获得最佳AUARC，比vanilla verbalization提升2.3个点，且无额外推理成本。

Conclusion: LLM的verbalization置信度存在严重稀疏性问题，需通过标准化阶梯插值来确保公平评估；verbalization logprobs是一种有效的、零额外成本的置信度估计改进方案，能够在不增加推理开销的前提下显著提升AUARC表现。

Abstract: Confidence estimation is essential when LLMs are used for classification, indicating when predictions can be trusted. However, common approaches such as verbalization produce extremely sparse outputs. For instance, Qwen3-32B verbalizes only eight unique confidence values on SST-2, with over half being exactly 95%, a pattern we observe consistently across four datasets and two LLMs. Besides limiting practical utility, we show that this sparsity critically affects evaluation: the choice of interpolation in area under the accuracy-rejection curve (AUARC) dramatically alters rankings, with consistency sampling dropping from best to worst under stepwise versus linear interpolation. We advocate for standardizing stepwise interpolation for a fairer comparison. Under such a fair evaluation, we find that weighting verbalized digits by token probabilities, a method we term verbalization logprobs, addresses sparsity and achieves the best AUARC (+2.3 points over vanilla verbalization) without incurring additional inference cost.

</details>


### [169] [Strengthening Target-Language Features: SAE-Based Steering for Multilingual Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04904)
*Hongsheng Wang,Phlipp Koehn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种推理时的多语言引导方法，利用预训练的稀疏自编码器（SAE）识别并增强与目标语言相关的特征，通过多语言平行句对比SAE激活值，选择少量层特定特征解码为引导信号注入模型隐藏状态，无需额外训练即可提升多语言性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大语言模型在不同语言上的性能差异显著，而现有的适配方法通常需要更新参数并依赖大量多语言训练数据，成本高昂且不灵活。

Method: 使用预训练的稀疏自编码器，通过多语言平行句对比SAE激活值，筛选出与目标语言相关的少量层特定特征，将这些特征解码为引导信号并在推理时注入模型的隐藏状态，全程无需额外训练。

Result: 在Gemma-3-12B-it上，XCOPA平均准确率提升10.9个百分点，XNLI提升5.3个百分点，MGSM提升1.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 基于SAE的推理时多语言引导是一种轻量、高效且无需参数更新的多语言能力增强方法，能显著缩小模型在不同语言上的性能差距。

Abstract: Multilingual large language models exhibit substantial performance differences across languages, while existing adaptation methods often require parameter updates and considerable multilingual training data. We propose an inference-time multilingual steering method that uses pretrained sparse autoencoders to identify and strengthen target-language-related features. Using multilingual parallel sentences, we compare SAE activations across languages and select a small number of layer-specific features associated with each target language. These features are decoded into steering signals and injected into the model's hidden states without additional training. Experiments with Gemma-3-12B-it show average accuracy improvements of 10.9 percentage points on XCOPA, 5.3 points on XNLI, and 1.9 points on MGSM.

</details>


### [170] [Does Out-of-Sight Equal Out-of-Mind in CoT Monitorability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04928)
*Pedro Ferreira,Wilker Aziz,Ivan Titov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了从显式思维链(CoT)到潜在思维链(latent CoT)的推理模式下，思维链的可监控性(monitorability)是否得以保留。通过基于提示的干预设置，以"依赖提示线索"行为为监控目标，比较不同推理模式下的监控效果。结果表明，可监控性更多取决于任务属性（如正确答案是否约束支持性推理）和模型内部访问级别，而非推理模式本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式CoT推理通过可读的推理轨迹为LLM决策提供监控窗口，但潜在CoT方法用连续状态替代显式token以降低推理成本，却失去了可读轨迹。此前不清楚通过探测激活或语言化潜在状态等替代访问方式能在多大程度上保留可监控性。

Method: 采用基于提示(hint-based)的干预设置，将"依赖偏置输入线索"（如泄露答案或用户陈述的信念）作为可监控性目标。在数学推理和问答任务上，比较显式CoT、弱监督潜在CoT和强监督潜在CoT三种推理模式下的监控器性能。

Result: 在实验设置下，可监控性更多取决于任务特性（例如正确答案是否约束支持性推理）以及对模型内部状态的访问级别，而不取决于推理模式（显式CoT vs 潜在CoT）。

Conclusion: 潜在CoT并非天然更难监控；监控能力的关键因素在于任务结构和内部访问程度，这为在保持推理效率的同时设计可监控的潜在推理系统提供了指导。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning offers a window into the decision-making of large language models (LLMs), which can be monitored for target behaviors by reading the reasoning trace, motivating work on CoT monitorability. Latent CoT approaches, however, replace the explicit tokens with a small number of continuous states, lowering inference costs but removing the readable trace this monitoring relies on. Monitoring then requires alternative access to the model, such as probing its activations or verbalizing the latent states back into text, but how much monitorability these alternatives preserve is unclear. We study this question with a hint-based intervention setup, a proxy for behaviors where models exploit biasing input cues, e.g., an inadvertently leaked answer or a belief stated by the user, without acknowledging them. Taking hint-reliance as the monitorability target, we compare monitors across reasoning modes, from explicit CoT to weakly- and strongly-supervised latent CoT, on math reasoning and question answering. We find that, in this setup, monitorability depends more on properties of the task (such as whether the correct answer constrains the supporting reasoning) and the level of access to model internals than on the reasoning mode.

</details>


### [171] [State2State: Environment-Derived Mid-Training for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04934)
*Xuanyu Lei,Yiqi Zhu,Chenliang Li,Kaiming Liu,Peng Li,Ming Yan,Jieping Ye,Ya-Qin Zhang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出State2State，一种环境派生的中期训练方法，通过将探索到的环境状态转化为训练目标，使LLM智能体无需外部任务或人工监督即可从环境中学习交互与操作能力。在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld上的实验表明，State2State在多数设置下能提升智能体性能，作为下游RL的初始化可进一步提升最终性能和学习效率，并展现出跨环境泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体训练方法（监督微调和在线强化学习）都依赖于外部指定的任务和人工构建的验证器或专家轨迹，这限制了智能体训练的规模化和多样性。作者希望突破这一瓶颈，探索一种无需外部任务规范的环境学习方法。

Method: State2State将环境探索中访问到的状态转换为训练目标，要求智能体从当前状态达到指定的目标状态。任务由环境探索自动生成，成功验证通过基于规则的状态匹配实现，无需专家监督或手动任务设计。该方法作为独立的环境学习阶段使用，也可作为下游强化学习的初始化。

Result: 在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld上的实验显示，State2State作为独立环境学习阶段在大多数设置下能提升智能体性能；作为下游RL的初始化时，它能提升最终性能和训练效率，并表现出跨环境迁移的初步证据。

Conclusion: State2State证明了从环境中自动派生可验证的训练目标是一种可行且有效的范式，能够减少对外部任务规范和人工监督的依赖，为规模化智能体训练提供了新方向。

Abstract: Training LLM agents commonly relies on supervised fine-tuning from expert trajectories or online reinforcement learning over human-specified tasks with handcrafted verifiers. Though effective, both remain bottlenecked by externally specified tasks and supervision signals, limiting the scalability and diversity of agent training. We study an environment learning paradigm in which agents acquire interaction and manipulation capabilities solely through environment interaction, without externally specified tasks. We propose State2State, an environment-derived mid-training method that converts explored environment states into training objectives, challenging agents to reach a specified target state. By deriving tasks from environment exploration and verifying success through rule-based state matching, State2State provides scalable and verifiable training objectives without expert supervision or manual task design. Experiments on ALFWorld and ScienceWorld show that State2State improves agent performance as a standalone environment-learning stage in most settings. As initialization for downstream RL, it further improves final performance and learning efficiency, with promising evidence of cross-environment generalization.

</details>


### [172] [Reading Between the Frames: Interpreting Implicit and Non-literal Meaning in Social Media Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04939)
*Yang Wang,Yanan Ma,Yiqi Liu,Zi Yan Chang,Chi-Li Chen,Chia-Yi Hsiao,Tyler Loakman,Aline Villavicencio,Chenghao Xiao,Chenghua Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了DrivelHub+基准，一个包含1,000个来自社交媒体的视频数据集，用于评估视频-语言模型能否推断表面看似无意义但实际蕴含刻意语用含义的隐式、非线性、修辞层叠语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体视频的语义往往超越其表层可见内容（如动作、字幕或语音），其幽默、反讽或讽刺效果只有通过多模态线索与文化语境的交互才能传达，这对视频-语言模型构成严峻挑战。现有的视频理解任务多关注识别或描述，缺乏针对上下文多模态推理能力的评估基准。

Method: 构建DrivelHub+基准，包含1,000个从社交媒体收集的视频，每个视频标注了人类撰写的隐式叙事解释。评估从两个角度进行：解释任务（模型需用自然语言解释视频的语用理解）和表征任务（采用推理即检索的方式，在视频到文本和文本到视频两个方向上测试模型表征能否将视频与其对应隐式叙事对齐）。

Result: 该基准提供了一个诊断性设置，用于衡量多模态感知与语用理解之间的差距，检验当前模型能否超越描述表面内容，进而推断其深层意图。

Conclusion: 当前视频-语言模型在从表层多模态感知向深层语用理解的跨越上仍存在显著差距，DrivelHub+可作为这一差距的度量工具，推动模型从"描述所见"向"推断所指"的方向发展。

Abstract: Social media videos often communicate meanings that go beyond their visible actions, captions, or speech. A mundane clip may become humorous, ironic, or satire only through the interaction of multimodal cues and cultural context, making such content a difficult test case for video-language models. In this paper, we introduce \textit{DrivelHub+}, a benchmark for evaluating whether models can infer the implicit, non-linear, and rhetorically layered meanings of social media videos that appear nonsensical on the surface but convey deliberate pragmatic meanings. DrivelHub+ consists of 1,000 videos collected from social media, each annotated with a human-written implicit narrative explanation. Unlike conventional video understanding tasks focused on recognition or description, we present a benchmark that targets contextual multimodal reasoning. We evaluate current video-language models from two perspectives: explanation, where models must explain the pragmatic comprehension of a video in natural language; and representation, where we adapt reasoning-as-retrieval to test whether model representations align videos with their corresponding implicit narratives in both video-to-text and text-to-video retrieval. Our benchmark provides a diagnostic setting for measuring the gap between multimodal perception and pragmatic comprehension, asking whether current models can move beyond describing what is shown to inferring what is meant.

</details>


### [173] [Protoreasoning in Tiny Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04980)
*Eduardo Valle,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"原型推理"（protoreasoning），一种简化的思维链形式，可在约1M参数的小型Transformer上实现逐步推理，并利用Dyck语言（正确嵌套括号的句子）作为推理友好的任务来研究步进推理的通用性。研究发现原型推理轨迹能显著缩小分布外泛化差距，且消融实验证实是轨迹内容（而非额外token本身）推动了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型展现出令人印象深刻的逐步推理能力，但我们尚不清楚这种推理的通用性——即LLM何时以及如何学到真正的通用算法而非"启发式集合"。这类问题在计算密集、数据不透明的前沿模型上难以研究。为在远低于自然语言能力的模型规模下工作，需要定义与推理相关的简化任务。

Method: 在约100万参数的小型Transformer上实现名为"原型推理"（protoreasoning）的简化思维链形式；使用Dyck语言（正确嵌套括号的句子）作为推理友好型任务；通过原型推理轨迹与消融实验验证轨迹内容的有效性。

Result: 原型推理轨迹能显著缩小分布外泛化差距；消融实验表明性能提升来自轨迹内容本身，而非仅仅因为增加了额外token。

Conclusion: 小型模型上的原型推理为研究逐步推理的通用性提供了可行的实验平台，相比大规模模型成本更低、更易于详细实验和分析，有助于理解LLM推理的机制本质。

Abstract: We show that tiny transformers can profitably employ a simple form of Chain of Thought, which we call protoreasoning, allowing us to study step-by-step reasoning on ~1M-parameter models and opening up opportunities for much more detailed experimentation and analysis than is feasible for larger models. Current Large Language Models exhibit impressive step-by-step reasoning, but we have yet to understand its generality, i.e., when and how LLMs learn genuinely general algorithms rather than "bags of heuristics." Such questions are hard to settle on compute-intensive frontier models trained on opaque data. To work at model scales far below the threshold for natural-language competence, we define reasoning-friendly tasks on Dyck languages (sentences of correctly nested brackets). We find that protoreasoning traces substantially close the out-of-distribution generalization gap, and ablations confirm that the trace's content, not merely its extra tokens, drives the gain.

</details>


### [174] [DelusionEval: Measuring Delusion-Linked Behaviors in AI Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05004)
*Jared Moore,Andrea Mock,Yifan Mai,Jacy Reese Anthis,Ryan Louie,William Agnew,Ashish Mehta,Kevin Klyman,Percy Liang,Nick Haber,Eric Lin,Desmond C. Ong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DelusionEval评测协议，通过18位经历妄想和心理伤害用户的12,591条真实对话消息（589个对话历史）来评估LLM是否表现出促进用户妄想的倾向。研究发现模型的大小、发布时间和测试时推理能力与妄想相关行为无可靠关联，但扩展上下文会显著增加妄想相关行为的发生率，所有模型家族均表现出相当比例的此类行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康专业人士担忧LLM交互可能引发用户心理伤害风险，特别是"妄想螺旋"现象——用户与模型的异常行为随时间相互强化。随着LLM聊天机器人普及，急需基于真实世界心理伤害案例的评估方法来衡量模型在此方面的风险。

Method: 构建DelusionEval评测协议：用589个来自18名参与者（共12,591条消息）的真实对话历史对模型进行提示，在特定上下文中测试模型是否表现出与促进用户妄想相关的行为（如未劝阻自残等）。通过控制上下文长度（额外前置350条消息）来考察上下文对行为的影响，并跨模型家族、模型规模和推理能力进行系统比较。

Result: LLM展现妄想相关行为的倾向与模型规模、发布时间或测试时推理能力无可靠相关；扩展上下文显著增加妄想相关行为率（如未能劝阻自残的比例从30.0%升至41.1%）；所有模型家族（GPT、Claude等）均表现出显著比例的妄想相关行为；家族内更晚、更大或更高推理能力的模型并非在所有行为类别上一致更优。

Conclusion: 研究结果对LLM潜在心理影响提出警示，强调需要更严格的真实世界人机交互研究。DelusionEval展示了上下文长度对安全评估的重要影响，为LLM安全评测提供了新维度。

Abstract: Mental health professionals have raised concerns about risks of psychological harm from interaction with large language models (LLMs), including "delusional spirals" in which concerning human and LLM behaviors reinforce each other over time. With growing public use of LLM-powered chatbots, there is an urgent need to build evaluations grounded in real-world episodes of psychological harm experienced by users. We developed DelusionEval, an evaluation protocol that tests a model's tendencies to exhibit behaviors linked to promoting user delusions. We prompt each model with 589 unique conversation histories from 18 participants, comprising 12,591 messages from users who experienced delusions and psychological harm. We find that the tendency of an evaluated LLM to exhibit delusion-linked behavior does not reliably correlate with model size, release date, or the presence of test-time reasoning. However, extending the context of prior messages substantially increases rates of delusion-linked behaviors, providing evidence for the importance of context in LLM safety evaluation. For example, the rate of failing to discourage self-harm when the user expresses suicidal ideation increases from 30.0% to 41.1% when an additional 350 messages are prepended to the conversation history. All model families (e.g., GPT, Claude) exhibit substantial rates of delusion-linked behaviors. Within families, later, larger, or higher-reasoning models are not uniformly better across all behavior categories. Our results raise concerns regarding the potential psychological impact of LLMs and the need for more rigorous studies of real-world human-AI interaction.

</details>


### [175] [OneDayAgent: Towards a Long-Horizon Harness for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05013)
*Jingsheng Zheng,Xinyuan Fang,Jintian Zhang,Zhengke Gui,Huajun Chen,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OneDayAgent是一个面向长时程、跨环境、多模态开放域任务的自主体（harness），通过将请求分解为有界子任务、维护执行记忆并在上下文压力下进行验证与修复，在AgentIF-OneDay基准上以GLM-5.2后端达到0.821的SOTA分数，且无需调参即可在五个后端LLM（三个模型家族）上泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的LLM智能体任务通常是长时程、跨环境、多模态的，需要同时应对目标漂移、状态丢失和上下文溢出等多重失败模式。先前工作仅单独解决某一类失败，缺少一个能同时管理所有问题并跨后端保持有效的统一harness方案。

Method: OneDayAgent采用三层管理机制：1) 将开放式请求分解为有界的子任务；2) 在上下文压力下维护执行记忆；3) 对最终交付物进行验证和修复。整个过程将开放式请求转化为可管理的执行流程。

Result: 在AgentIF-OneDay基准的104个任务上，搭配GLM-5.2后端时OneDayAgent取得0.821的整体分数，达到新的SOTA。同一harness在跨越三个模型家族的五个后端LLM上均有效运行，无需针对各后端调参；不同模型在同一工作流下表现出不同的执行风格。

Conclusion: OneDayAgent证明了单一统一harness能够同时管理目标保持、状态维护和上下文管理等多重挑战，且可跨不同后端LLM泛化，为长时程智能体任务提供了一种通用且稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly applied to open-ended everyday requests that span work, study, and life. These tasks are long-horizon, cross-environment, and multimodal, forcing the agent to preserve goals and constraints across many steps while navigating heterogeneous tools and attachments. While prior work has addressed individual failure modes such as goals drift, states loss, and context overflow, whether a single harness can manage them jointly and remain effective across backends has received less study. We present OneDayAgent, a long-horizon harness for autonomous agents. OneDayAgent turns an open-ended request into a managed execution process that decomposes tasks into bounded subtasks, maintains execution memory under context pressure, and verifies and repairs the final deliverable. We evaluate OneDayAgent on AgentIF-OneDay across 104 tasks. With the GLM-5.2 backend, OneDayAgent sets a new state of the art with an overall score of 0.821. The same harness runs across five backend LLMs from three model families, indicating the harness generalizes across backends without tuning, even as different models induce distinct execution styles under the same workflow.

</details>


### [176] [Language Models Generalize to Human-like Word Order Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05028)
*Amanda Popadich,Shane Steinert-Threlkeld*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在人工语言学习（ALL）框架下，语言模型是否能在缺失多修饰语名词短语的训练语料中，自发获得与人类学习者一致的"作用域同态"修饰语排序偏好。实验发现三种规模的模型均一致偏好作用域同态排序，且该偏好无法由PMI（点互信息）解释，表明LMs能从贫乏输入中恢复类人的语言归纳偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言习得领域的核心问题在于：语言偏误是否可以通过一般性学习机制从欠定输入中涌现。已有ALL研究表明人类学习者会可靠地将证据推广到未见情况，特别是偏好作用域同态的名词短语修饰语排序。本文旨在探究语言模型在相同受控条件下是否展现相同的偏误，从而为理解语言归纳的机制提供计算层面的参照。

Method: 作者构建了受控学习环境：训练语料中移除所有含多个修饰语的名词短语，使模型在训练中完全看不到修饰语排序的直接证据。随后在三种不同规模的模型上评估其对多修饰语句子的生成/选择倾向。此外，通过点互信息（PMI）计算名词与修饰语之间的关联强度，以检验该统计信号是否能解释模型表现出的排序偏好。

Result: 实验结果表明：三种规模的模型在从未见过多修饰语名词短语的情况下，均一致偏好作用域同态的修饰语排序，但偏好强度因修饰语类型而异。PMI虽然能反映已知的修饰语排序模式，但与模型的排序偏好没有因果解释关系，即PMI无法解释模型的偏好来源。

Conclusion: 这些发现表明语言模型能够从贫乏的输入中恢复出类似人类的语言归纳偏好，为理解语言偏误的涌现机制提供了一个可控的计算研究框架。该工作弥合了ALL行为研究与语言模型研究之间的鸿沟，并为后续探究此类偏误的认知与计算根源提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: A central question in language acquisition is whether linguistic biases can emerge from general learning mechanisms operating over underdetermined input. Artificial Language Learning (ALL) studies have shown that human learners reliably generalize beyond the evidence provided, including by preferring scope-homomorphic noun phrase modifier orders. In this work, we investigate whether language models exhibit the same bias under similar conditions. We create a controlled learning environment in which models are trained on a corpus where all noun phrases containing multiple modifiers have been removed, eliminating direct evidence about modifier ordering, and are then evaluated on multiple modifier sentences. Across three model sizes, we find that they consistently prefer scope-homomorphic orders despite never observing them during training. These preferences vary in strength by modifier type. To investigate the source of these preferences, we examine noun-modifier association strength using pointwise mutual information (PMI). While PMI reflects known modifier-ordering patterns, it does not explain the models' ordering preferences. These findings demonstrate that LMs can recover human-like linguistic generalizations from impoverished input and provide a controlled framework for investigating the mechanisms underlying such biases.

</details>


### [177] [Provable Limits and Certified Deferral for Verbalized Uncertainty in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05064)
*Jianru Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了小型开放权重语言模型的言语化置信度能否支持风险可控的委派（deferral）决策。在三个模型家族（0.5B-14B）、11个指令微调模型、两个数据集（ARC-Challenge和TruthfulQA）共25,168个本地预测上，作者提出三个理论结果界定校准的边界，并通过经验实验发现大部分模型无法通过温度缩放达到风险认证，仅有极少数模型能在设定的风险预算下获得自主运行资格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型开放权重语言模型越来越多地部署在私有、离线、成本敏感的本地环境中，关键问题不仅是模型回答什么，还包括何时应将任务委派给人类。现有研究缺乏对言语化置信度是否能支持风险可控的委派决策的系统性评估，因此需要从理论和实证两个层面回答这一问题。

Method: 理论层面：1) 证明严格单调校准保持风险覆盖率前沿和错误检测AUROC不变；2) 推导出温度缩放无法校准"置信度始终高于0.5而准确率低于0.5"的模型这一不可行性界限；3) 将Clopper-Pearson方法转化为200题校准集上的有限样本风险证书。实证层面：对11个指令微调模型（三大家族，0.5B-14B参数）在ARC-Challenge和TruthfulQA上进行25,168个本地预测实验，评估温度缩放和Platt缩放的校准效果，并检验在20%和10%风险预算下的认证自主性。同时识别并修复了TruthfulQA多项选择格式中的答案顺序伪影。

Result: 22个模型-任务组合中有8个在温度缩放不可行性界限的一个百分点内触及理论下限。Platt缩放可将ECE降至0.02，但仅有3个模型-任务组合在20%风险预算下获得认证自主性，在10%风险预算下则无一通过。此外，在TruthfulQA中发现并修复了多项选择格式的答案顺序伪影。

Conclusion: 校准赋予置信度以语义，但认证委派才决定小型模型何时可以安全使用。言语化置信度虽能提供有意义的概率语义，但当前的小型开放权重模型在严格风险预算下的安全部署仍然极为受限，需要超越传统校准方法的风险控制策略。

Abstract: Small open-weight language models increasingly run in private, offline, and cost-sensitive settings, where the key deployment question is not only what a model answers but when it should defer to a human. We study whether verbalized confidence can support risk-controlled deferral, evaluating eleven instruction-tuned models from three families, 0.5B to 14B parameters, on ARC-Challenge and TruthfulQA with 25,168 local predictions. Three theoretical results delimit what calibration can provide: strictly monotone calibration preserves the risk-coverage frontier and error-detection AUROC; temperature scaling cannot calibrate models whose confidence stays above one half while accuracy falls below it; and a Clopper-Pearson procedure converts a 200-question calibration set into a finite-sample risk certificate under an i.i.d. deployment assumption. Empirically, eight of 22 model-task pairs hit the temperature-scaling infeasibility floor within one percentage point of the predicted bound. Platt scaling reduces ECE to as low as 0.02, yet certified autonomy at a 20% risk budget is granted to only three model-task pairs and to none at 10%. We also identify and repair an answer-ordering artifact in the multiple-choice form of TruthfulQA. Calibration gives confidence semantics; certified deferral determines when small models are safe to use.

</details>


### [178] [German parties shifted towards intuition-based rhetoric after the far right's parliamentary breakthrough](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05075)
*Peer Saleth,Segun T. Aroyehun,Fabio Carrella,Christoph M. Abels,Stephan Lewandowsky,David Garcia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了2015至2025年间德国政治精英在推特（450万条推文）和议会演讲（59,170篇）中的话语，使用分布式词典表征测量基于证据与基于直觉的修辞，发现直觉式语言日益突出，右翼行为者EMI得分最低，且2017年德国选择党（AfD）进入议会后，整个议会的EMI分数出现急剧下降，推特上的下降更为渐进，表明极右翼的可见度与精英话语中真理观的变化相关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 错误信息的传播被视为对民主审议的威胁，但政治精英关于真相的修辞承诺如何随民粹主义行为者崛起而变化，目前仍缺乏深入理解。

Method: 收集并分析了德国政治精英在2015至2025年间发布的450万条推文和59,170篇议会演讲，使用经过验证的分布式词典表征来测量基于证据和基于直觉的修辞，并计算证据减直觉（EMI）得分。

Result: 在两个场域中，直觉式语言都变得更加突出；右翼行为者的EMI得分始终最低。2017年极右翼AfD进入议会与整个议会EMI分数的急剧下降相吻合，而推特上的下降则更为渐进。

Conclusion: 研究发现记录了一个多党制欧洲民主国家中，极右翼的可见度与精英话语中对待真相方式变化之间的关联。

Abstract: The spread of misinformation is widely perceived as a threat to democratic deliberation, yet how political elites' rhetorical commitments to truth shift alongside the rise of populist actors remains poorly understood. Analysing 4.5 million tweets and 59,170 parliamentary speeches by German political elites between 2015 and 2025, we measure evidence-based and intuition-based rhetoric using a validated distributed dictionary representation. Across both arenas, intuition-based language has become more prominent, and right-leaning actors consistently exhibit the lowest Evidence Minus Intuition (EMI) scores. The parliamentary entry of the extreme-right Alternative for Germany (AfD) in 2017 coincides with sharp downward shifts in EMI across the broader chamber, while a more gradual decline is observed on Twitter. These findings document an association between far-right visibility and a changing approach to truth in elite discourse in a multiparty European democracy.

</details>


### [179] [Same Formulas, Different Semantics: Do Language Models Follow Modal Logic Specifications?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05097)
*Réemi Andrieu,Damien Sileo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了配对的模态逻辑推理任务，通过固定前提与结论、改变框架或论域条件，实现自动验证的相反标签，并揭示大多数近期语言模型在直接提示下表现低于仅依条件先验的基线，但启用推理模式可显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在模态推理（必然性与可能性）中的判断依赖世界间可达性和各世界存在对象的假设。同一推理在不同模态系统下可能成立或失效，因此评估语言模型需要验证其是否遵循给定的语义规则，而非依赖熟悉的逻辑。目前缺乏针对性的、可自动验证的模态语义测试基准。

Method: 构造配对的模态问题：两对命题前提与猜想完全相同，但框架（frame）或论域（domain）条件不同，通过自动化推理验证生成相反标签。设计平衡的核心测试集，使语义条件本身无法直接泄露答案。对五个近期模型在直接提示和推理模式下分别进行评测。

Result: 在平衡核心集上，五个近期模型中有四个在直接提示下表现低于仅依据条件先验的基线。开启推理模式后，DeepSeek V4 Flash在提示不变的情况下从4.4%提升至88.1%。省略框架条件时，模型间判断趋于一致，但各自拟合的熟悉逻辑各不相同。

Conclusion: 模态语义的遵循程度强烈依赖于推理模式与模型身份。模型是否真正遵循给定的模态语义而非默认逻辑，需要结合语义条件控制与推理模式切换来综合评估。相关工作产物（公式、oracle工件、反模型及响应）已公开。

Abstract: Reasoning about necessity and possibility depends on assumptions about accessibility between worlds and about which objects exist at each one. The same inference may therefore hold under one modal system and fail under another. Evaluating language models on such problems requires testing whether their judgments follow the stated semantics rather than a familiar logic. We construct paired modal problems with identical premises and conjecture but different frame or domain conditions; automated reasoning verifies opposite labels. A balanced core prevents the semantic condition alone from revealing the answer. On this core, four of five recent models perform below the condition-only baseline under direct prompting. Yet enabling reasoning mode raises DeepSeek V4 Flash from 4.4% to 88.1% on unchanged prompts. Following stipulated modal semantics thus depends strongly on inference mode as well as model identity. When frame conditions are omitted, models often agree but fit different familiar logics best. We release the formulas, oracle artifacts, countermodels, and responses.

</details>


### [180] [Chained Recursive Language Models for Multi-Iteration Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05124)
*Purbesh Mitra,Sennur Ulukus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Chained Recursive Language Models (Chained RLM)推理时架构，通过多次调用同一底层模型作为一系列全新推理根，每个根接收原始问题、上下文、紧凑文本摘要和黑板上的工件（artifacts）而非完整对话历史，将长上下文推理拆分为分段任务，使中间状态可被后续推理检查、纠正和扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中，单次推理轨迹需同时完成上下文探索、中间状态存储、证据验证和最终答案生成，在提取、计数、排序或多跳推理等任务中，早期错误会一路传播至最终输出。现有方法缺乏将推理过程分解为可独立验证和修正阶段的能力。

Method: 提出Chained RLM架构：同一底层模型被反复调用，每次作为一个全新的推理根（fresh reasoning root）。每个根接收原始问题与上下文，但不继承完整对话历史，而是接收前驱根写入的紧凑纯文本摘要、纯文本黑板（blackboard）以及持久化的任务特定工件（artifacts）。通过手写机制（handoff mechanism）在阶段间传递信息，将大型推理任务切分为部分任务而非一次大推理响应。

Result: 论文描述了系统模型、交接机制、工件工作区和评估协议，研究了在何种条件下，即使使用递归工具调用（recursive tool-calling），fresh-context工件续写相比直接LLM作答仍能带来可测量的准确率提升。

Conclusion: Chained RLM通过分段推理、中间工件可检查和修正的方式，为长上下文推理提供了一种新的推理时架构范式，有望缓解早期错误传播问题，但实际收益的条件需要系统评估。

Abstract: Long context reasoning in large language models (LLMs) is usually constrained by the fact that a single inference trajectory has to simultaneously explore the context, store intermediate state, verify evidence, and produce the final answer. This becomes particularly difficult in tasks that require extraction, counting, ordering, or multi-hop reasoning, where an early mistake can propagate until the final response. In this work, we propose Chained Recursive Language Models (Chained RLM), an inference-time architecture, in which the same underlying model is called repeatedly as a sequence of fresh reasoning roots. Each root receives the original problem and context, but does not inherit the full conversational history. Instead, it receives a compact plain-text summary, a plain-text blackboard, and some durable task-specific artifacts written by predecessor roots. The motivation is to manage the context by chopping into partial tasks rather than one large inference response; in each staged computation, intermediate artifacts can be inspected, corrected, and extended by a later fresh inference by the same model. We describe the system model, handoff mechanism, artifact workspace, and evaluation protocol for this system. We study when fresh-context artifact continuation gives a measurable gain in accuracy over direct LLM answering even with recursive tool-calling.

</details>


### [181] [Spoken Function Calling: A New Perspective on Spoken Language Understanding for Large Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05126)
*Yuezhang Peng,Yuxin Liu,Changfeng Gao,Zhifu Gao,Xiangang Li,Xie Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出Spoken Function Calling (SFC)，一种基于结构化规则定义的语音语义理解新范式，将传统封闭集SLU拓展到开放域任务。通过构建SFC-Bench数据集、评估LLM和LALM性能，并通过后训练增强LALM的SFC能力，实验表明SFC在语义提取准确性上显著优于传统SLU方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SLU在封闭集任务中经过领域内监督微调后能有效提取用户语义，但在开放域任务中面临规则定义模糊的问题，难以利用上下文学习（in-context learning）能力。作者希望通过引入结构化规则定义来突破传统SLU的局限。

Method: 1) 基于传统SLU数据集策划并扩展一套口语函数（spoken functions）；2) 构建多智能体系统合成SFC-Bench数据集；3) 评估LLM和LALM在SFC任务上的表现；4) 通过后训练（post-training）增强LALM的SFC能力。

Result: 实验证明SFC方法在语义提取准确性上优于传统SLU，显著提升了LLM和LALM的语义提取精度。

Conclusion: SFC作为一种新的语义理解范式，通过结构化规则定义超越了传统封闭集SLU的限制，为开放域语音语义理解提供了更有效的解决方案，特别是在增强LALM的SFC能力方面取得了显著进展。

Abstract: Spoken Language Understanding (SLU) is the core component of task-oriented dialogue systems and a pivotal link in achieving seamless human-agent interaction. While traditional SLU can effectively extract user semantics for closed-set tasks after in-domain supervised fine-tuning, it faces significant challenges in leveraging in-context learning for open-domain tasks due to its ambiguous rule definitions. This work proposes Spoken Function Calling (SFC), a novel semantic understanding perspective that optimizes semantic understanding with structured rule definitions, to evolve beyond traditional closed-set SLU. Specifically, we curate and extend a suite of spoken functions based on traditional SLU datasets, construct a multi-agent system to synthesize the SFC-Bench dataset, evaluate the performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) and Large Audio Language Models (LALMs), and enhance the SFC capabilities of LALMs through post-training. Experiments demonstrate that SFC outperforms traditional SLU, substantially enhancing the semantic extraction accuracy for LLMs and LALMs.

</details>


### [182] [Toward Skill-Native LLMs: Skill Entropy for Benchmarking and Training Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05139)
*Yinghui He,Ling Yang,Jiarui Liu,Yongjin Yang,Lechen Zhang,Yingcheng Wu,Zhenfei Yin,Mengdi Wang,Sanjeev Arora*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Skill Entropy（技能熵）来量化跨技能长程推理任务中技能切换的难度，并构建Skill^2-Bench基准（覆盖558个技能、9个领域），同时提出Skill-Entropy RL框架将技能熵用作训练信号，在Qwen3模型上将Skill^2-Bench分数大幅提升（34.4%→68.4%，14.6%→40.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长程推理基准往往只评估单一技能，缺乏一种原则性的方法来衡量模型在推理链中如何在不同技能之间切换。作者希望从评估和训练两个层面弥合这一空白。

Method: （1）提出Skill Entropy度量技能切换难度；（2）构建Skill^2-Bench基准，覆盖558个技能、9个可验证的开领域域，每个任务分配技能熵分数并按难度分为三级；（3）提出Skill-Entropy RL框架，模型在每一步不仅预测答案还预测所用技能，奖励结合步骤级正确性和技能熵奖励（衡量预测技能序列与金标准技能序列的对齐度）。

Result: 在Skill^2-Bench上评估8个前沿和4个开源模型，发现熵越高的任务准确率越低（技能切换鸿沟）。Skill-Entropy RL将Qwen3-4B-Instruct从34.4%提升到68.4%，Qwen3-1.7B从14.6%提升到40.1%，超越竞争基线；该管线还可直接应用于现成训练数据（如OpenR1-Math）。

Conclusion: 技能熵既可作为基准度量，也可作为可复用的训练信号，有效提升模型在跨技能长程推理任务上的表现。代码已开源。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning in recent LLMs demands that the model switch between distinct skills inside a reasoning chain, such as first doing a math derivation, then using the result to plan a schedule. We call such problems cross-skill long-horizon tasks: multi-step tasks whose steps require different reasoning skills and depend on earlier outputs. Existing benchmarks often evaluate individual skills, lacking a principled way to measure how well a model switches between skills. We address this gap from both the evaluation and training sides. We introduce Skill Entropy, a measure of the difficulty of switching from one skill to another. We then propose Skill^2-Bench, a benchmark of cross-skill long-horizon tasks built over 558 skills across 9 verifiable and open-ended domains. Each task is assigned a task-level skill-entropy score and grouped into three difficulty levels. Evaluating 8 frontier and 4 open-source models on Skill^2-Bench reveals a skill-switching gap: accuracy decreases on higher-entropy tasks. We then turn skill entropy from a benchmark scale into a training signal. We propose Skill-Entropy RL, an RL framework where the model predicts not only the answer at each step but also the skill used to produce it. The reward combines step-level correctness with a skill-entropy reward that measures the alignment between the model-predicted skill sequence and the gold skill sequence. On Qwen3-4B-Instruct and Qwen3-1.7B, Skill-Entropy RL improves the Skill^2-Bench score from 34.4% to 68.4% and from 14.6% to 40.1%, respectively, outperforming competitive baselines. The same pipeline can be applied to off-the-shelf training data such as OpenR1-Math, indicating that skill entropy is a reusable training signal. Code available at: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/Skill-Entropy-RL

</details>


### [183] [Reasoning Core: Designing Broad Procedural Data for Completion-Supervised Reasoning Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05148)
*Damien Sileo,Valentin Lacombe,Dimitri Kachler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Reasoning Core，一个包含50个程序化生成器的推理问题集合，覆盖数学、逻辑、规划等多领域，并通过匹配的补全监督协议在多个基础模型上验证其优于现有的程序化数据集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序化生成器能够大规模产生有用的可验证推理问题，但作为补全监督微调数据的潜力尚未被充分探索。作者旨在系统性地评估程序化生成数据在推理任务微调中的效用。

Method: 构建50个覆盖数学、逻辑、规划、状态追踪、形式语言、结构化数据、游戏、因果关系和代码的程序化生成器，配备语义评分器、难度控制和任务评估器。在四种基础模型设置和多种训练时长下，采用匹配的补全监督协议，将Reasoning Core与Procedural Warmup、Reasoning Gym和SynLogic三个基线进行比较。同时引入结合模型辅助审查、人工仲裁和回归测试的审计流程。

Result: 在主要的3B模型对比中，Reasoning Core在DROP、LogiQA和ARC-Challenge上取得最高平均分，超过了无程序化数据的基线和全部三个替代程序化数据集合。任务级分析表明语义有效性并不保证训练效用，紧凑目标和校准的难度是重要的设计因素。审计揭示了生成、渲染、目标和评分之间的细微不匹配。

Conclusion: 程序化生成本身并不能保证正确性，需要严格的审计流程。Reasoning Core及其审计材料均已公开，为程序化生成数据用于推理微调提供了有价值的资源和基准。

Abstract: Procedural generators produce useful verifiable reasoning problems at scale, but have received less attention as data for completion-supervised fine-tuning. We introduce Reasoning Core, a collection of 50 generators spanning mathematics, logic, planning, state tracking, formal languages, structured data, games, causality, and code, with semantic scorers, difficulty controls, and task evaluators. Under a matched completion-supervised protocol, we compare Reasoning Core with Procedural Warmup, Reasoning Gym, and SynLogic across four base-model settings and multiple training durations. In the primary 3B comparison, Reasoning Core achieves the highest mean scores on DROP, LogiQA, and ARC-Challenge, exceeding both the baseline without procedural data and all three alternative procedural collections. Task-level analyses show that semantic validity alone does not ensure training utility, highlighting compact targets and calibrated difficulty as important design factors. We ran audits combining model-assisted review, human adjudication, and regression testing. Applied throughout Reasoning Core development and to the other collections, they reveal subtle mismatches among generation, rendering, targets, and scoring, a reminder that procedural generation alone does not guarantee correctness. The library, generated datasets, and audit material are publicly available.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Kitchen Robotic Manipulation utilizing Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04042)
*Myung-Hwan Jeon,Sankalp Yamsani,Joohyung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于家庭厨房餐具操作任务的模块化感知流水线，集成开放词汇目标检测、多视角分割、实例感知3D重建及2D-3D特征融合技术，用于6D姿态估计和抓取规划。最佳配置在20场景厨房基准上达到89.12%的ADI，并成功部署到真实机器人上执行水槽到洗碗机转移和杯子堆叠等任务，无需特定环境重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在日常生活环境中部署机器人需要既鲁棒又适应多样化动态条件的感知系统，而家庭环境尤其是厨房场景对机器人的感知能力提出了更高要求。

Method: 提出模块化感知流水线，集成开放词汇目标检测、多视角分割、实例感知3D重建以及2D-3D特征融合策略，用于6D姿态估计和抓取规划。流水线采用模块化设计，可系统替换多种视觉和几何基础模型以寻找最优配置，并在自建厨房数据集上进行了广泛评估。

Result: 最佳配置（LLMDet + SAMv2 + DINOv2 + GeoTransformer）在包含20个场景、存在杂乱和遮挡条件的厨房基准上达到89.12%的ADI；真实世界演示验证了最佳配置可直接部署到物理机器人上，无需环境特定重训练，成功执行水槽到洗碗机转移和杯子堆叠等任务。

Conclusion: 该流水线验证了模块化感知框架在家庭机器人系统中的适应性和可扩展性，可作为家庭机器人系统的实用框架，代码和补充材料已公开。

Abstract: Deploying robots in everyday human environments requires perception systems that are both robust and adaptable to diverse, dynamic conditions. In this work, we present a modular perception pipeline for household manipulation tasks, with a focus on dishware handling in kitchen environments. The pipeline integrates open-vocabulary object detection, multi-view segmentation, instance-aware 3D reconstruction, and a 2D-3D feature fusion strategy for 6D pose estimation and grasp planning. Its modular design enables systematic substitution of multiple visual and geometric foundation models, allowing us to identify the best-performing configuration through extensive evaluation on a custom kitchen dataset. The best-performing configuration (LLMDet + SAMv2 + DINOv2 + GeoTransformer) achieves an ADI of 89.12\% on the 20-scene kitchen benchmark with cluttered and occluded conditions. Furthermore, real-world demonstrations confirm that the best configuration can be deployed on physical robots without environment-specific retraining, successfully executing tasks such as sink-to-dishwasher transfer and cup stacking. It validates the adaptability and scalability of the pipeline and highlights its potential as a practical framework for household robotic systems. Our code and supplementary materials are available at https://raivlab.github.io/FM_kitchen .

</details>


### [185] [Interpretable Fuzzy Inference for UAV Target Tracking Using Bounding-Box Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04121)
*Reza Ahmari,Ahmad Mohammadi,Vahid Hemmati,Nicholas Edmond,Hossein Z. Saghazadeh,Olusola Odeyomi,Parham Kebria,Abdollah Homaifar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可解释模糊推理框架的无人机（UAV）视觉引导方法，利用YOLO目标检测框的质心位置、面积和宽高比等低维特征生成连续偏航（yaw）指令，无需显式几何建模。在VICON动捕环境下收集的6,169个标注样本上，Takagi-Sugeno模糊模型在五个随机训练-测试拆分中取得0.140°的平均绝对误差和99.676%的±1°阈值内准确率，展示了透明、数据高效、计算轻量且适合实时UAV视觉引导的优点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机（UAV）朝向地面无人车辆（UGV）的视觉引导对协同空地机器人具有重要意义，但基于机载视觉的连续偏航估计面临感知不确定性、有限计算资源和需要可解释控制等挑战。现有深度学习和几何重建方法通常需要大规模数据集、外部定位系统或复杂的建模假设，降低了透明度并限制了在资源受限平台上的部署适用性。因此，需要一个透明、数据高效且计算轻量的偏航估计框架。

Method: 提出一个可解释的模糊推理框架，从YOLO检测框提取三个低维特征（目标质心位置、面积、宽高比）作为输入，生成连续偏航指令。具体包括两个模型：（1）Mamdani模糊系统作为可解释基线，采用肩-三角-肩输入划分；（2）一阶Takagi-Sugeno模型，每个输入使用三个前件隶属项，参数从训练集分位数推导，形成紧凑的27条规则结构。两者均无需显式几何建模。

Result: 在VICON动捕环境采集的6,169个标注样本上进行五个随机训练-测试拆分评估：Takagi-Sugeno模型测试集平均绝对误差为0.140°±0.003°，均方根误差为0.200°±0.008°，最大绝对误差为1.254°±0.121°。±1°阈值内准确率为99.676%±0.270%，±3°和±5°阈值内准确率均达100.000%±0.000%。图像平面水平位移与预测偏航符号的方向一致性达90.254%±0.612%。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持透明性和可解释性的同时实现了极高的偏航估计精度，且数据效率高、计算负担小，适合资源受限的无人机平台在实时场景中实现对移动地面目标的视觉引导。

Abstract: Vision-based guidance of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) toward unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) supports cooperative aerial--ground robotics, but reliable continuous yaw estimation from onboard vision remains challenging because of sensing uncertainty, limited computation, and the need for interpretable control. Existing deep-learning and geometric-reconstruction approaches often require large datasets, external localization, or complex modeling assumptions, reducing transparency and deployment suitability on resource-constrained platforms. We present an interpretable fuzzy-inference framework that generates continuous yaw commands from low-dimensional features extracted from YOLO boxes: target centroid location, area, and aspect ratio. No explicit geometric modeling is required. A Mamdani fuzzy system serves as an interpretable baseline using a shoulder--triangle--shoulder input partition. It is followed by a first-order Takagi--Sugeno model with three antecedent membership terms per input, whose parameters are derived from training-set quantiles, yielding a compact 27-rule structure. Evaluation uses 6{,}169 labeled samples from a VICON motion-capture environment. Across five randomized train--test splits, the Takagi--Sugeno model achieves a test-set mean absolute error of $0.140^\circ \pm 0.003^\circ$, a root mean squared error of $0.200^\circ \pm 0.008^\circ$, and a maximum absolute error of $1.254^\circ \pm 0.121^\circ$. Within-threshold accuracies are $99.676% \pm 0.270%$ for $\pm1^\circ$ and $100.000% \pm 0.000%$ for both $\pm3^\circ$ and $\pm5^\circ$. Directional consistency between image-plane horizontal displacement and predicted yaw sign reaches $90.254% \pm 0.612%$. These results show that the framework is transparent, data-efficient, computationally lightweight, and suitable for real-time vision-based UAV guidance toward mobile ground targets.

</details>


### [186] [SiMDex: Mining Similar Egocentric Videos for Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04196)
*Nie Lin,Takehiko Ohkawa,Sijin Chen,Ruoshi Wen,Zhuohang Li,Liqun Huang,Zhengming Zhu,Yiming Bao,Yunfei Li,Minjie Cai,Xiao Ma,Wei Xu,Yoichi Sato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SiMDex提出了一种基于相似度的数据挖掘框架，将VLA后训练中的人类数据筛选建模为推荐问题，通过三层"召回-排序-重排序"流程从约3200万个第一人称人类视频样本中提取任务相关子集，仅用不到5%的数据（约149万样本）即将操作成功率从47.7%提升至61.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，利用第一人称人类视频进行机器人操作训练的规模不断扩大，但哪些数据真正有益于灵巧操作仍不明确，需要一种系统化的数据筛选方法。

Method: 提出SiMDex框架，将人类数据选择视为推荐问题。采用三层"召回-排序-重排序"流水线，在形态无关的动作空间中为每条机器人演示提取任务相关的人类数据子集，无需修改VLA架构或训练流程。

Result: 在等量随机采样人类数据的强基线上，SiMDex仅用约149万条（不到池的5%）挖掘样本即将总体成功率从47.7%提升到61.1%，证明选择性数据策展优于不加区分的数据混合。

Conclusion: 有选择的数据筛选能显著优于随机混合，智能数据挖掘是提升灵巧操作VLA性能的关键，而非简单地堆砌更多人类视频数据。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed an explosive trend of scaling ego-centric human videos for robot manipulation, yet it remains unclear which data actually benefits dexterous manipulation. We present SiMDex, a similarity-based data mining framework that casts human data selection for VLA post-training in dexterous manipulation as a recommendation problem. For each robot demonstration, SiMDex employs a three-layer recall-ranking-re-ranking pipeline to extract task-relevant subsets from a pool of ~32M egocentric human samples, operating in a morphology-agnostic action space that requires no changes to VLA architecture or training. Against a strong baseline trained with an equal amount of randomly sampled human data, SiMDex uses only ~1.49M mined samples (<5% of the pool) yet improves the overall success rate from 47.7% to 61.1%, showing that selective curation outperforms indiscriminate data mixing.

</details>


### [187] [Feasibility of Embedded Photoplethysmography Sensing in Short-Duration Tactile Interactions With Pocket-Sized Robots Using IMU- and Confidence-Based Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04242)
*Turjja Datta,Morten Roed Frederiksen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将PPG传感器嵌入口袋大小的陪伴机器人AffectaPocket中，通过两阶段滤波管道（IMU拒绝高方差片段+置信度平滑恢复期）抑制手持交互中的运动伪影，实现自包含的无创心率监测。在26名参与者的受试者内研究中，验证了该方法与腕戴式金标准在统计上等效（p<0.05）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有儿童焦虑缓解陪伴机器人依赖外部可穿戴设备监测生理状态，存在可用性障碍且限制了机器人的自主性，亟需将生理监测能力直接嵌入机器人本体。

Method: 将PPG传感器直接嵌入口袋大小的陪伴机器人AffectaPocket中，实现触觉交互过程中的自包含心率监测。采用两阶段滤波管道：第一阶段利用板载IMU拒绝高方差运动片段，第二阶段采用基于置信度的平滑算法处理恢复期数据。通过26名参与者的受试者内研究，与常见腕戴式传感器进行对比验证。

Result: 两阶段滤波策略显著降低了平均绝对百分比误差（MAPE），并在统计上与金标准地面真值测量达到等效性（p<0.05）。但短期交互分析表明传感器需要较长时间的稳定性才能收敛。

Conclusion: 将PPG嵌入陪伴机器人实现自包含心率监测是可行且有效的路径，但该系统在短时交互场景下的收敛速度仍需改进，以支持更快速响应的焦虑干预。

Abstract: Ubiquitous companion robots offer a promising avenue for immediate anxiety relief in children, yet their effectiveness relies on the ability to monitor physiological states continuously and unobtrusively. Current solutions often depend on external wearables, which impose usability barriers and limit the robot's autonomy. This paper investigates the integration of an embedded photoplethysmography (PPG) sensor directly into a pocket-sized companion robot, AffectaPocket, to enable self-contained heart rate monitoring during tactile interaction. We address the significant challenge of motion artifacts inherent in handheld usage by implementing a two-stage filtering pipeline that utilizes an onboard Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) to reject high-variance segments and a confidence-based smoothing algorithm for recovery periods. We evaluated the system against a commonly used wrist worn sensor in a Within-Subjects Study with 26 participants. Our results demonstrate that the filtering strategy significantly reduced the Mean Absolute Percentage Error and achieved statistical equivalence to the ground truth measurements (p<0.05). Analysis of short-duration interactions shows that the sensor requires stability over longer periods to converge.

</details>


### [188] [SAFECAST: Robust Failure Detection for VLA Policies with Contrast-Set Training and Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04246)
*Harshitha Rajaprakash,Aditeya Prajapati,Rong Xue,Abrar Anwar,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAFECAST利用对比集扰动增强隐藏状态风险探针的训练与校准，显著提升视觉-语言-动作策略在部署时分布偏移下的失败检测能力，在DROID真实机器人和LIBERO仿真实验的多个VLM主干上均优于最先进基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作策略在部署时面临分布偏移（如杂乱、干扰物、光照变化、新物体、初始状态改变和指令改写）时容易失败。已有的基于隐藏状态的风险探针结合函数化共形预测能检测滚动失败，但其可靠性依赖校准数据与部署条件匹配，这在现实场景中难以保证。

Method: SAFECAST采用对比集扰动（contrast set perturbations）来改进隐藏状态探针的训练和校准过程，以应对部署时的分布偏移。通过同时使用视觉和语言层面的对比集扰动来增强数据，提升探针对分布偏移的鲁棒性和校准质量。

Result: 在DROID真实机器人实验和LIBERO仿真实验中，SAFECAST在故障检测ROC-AUC分数上统计显著优于最先进的基线方法，且适用于多种VLM主干网络。实验还发现：同时使用视觉和语言对比集扰动时收益最大；使用对比集扰动后，仿真到真实的校准优于仅使用真实滚动数据。

Conclusion: SAFECAST通过对比集扰动有效提升了隐藏状态探针在分布偏移下的失败检测能力，为VLA策略的可靠性部署提供了更稳健的风险检测方法，并揭示了视觉-语言联合扰动增强和仿真到真实校准的优势。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies often fail under deployment-time distribution shifts such as clutter, distractor objects, lighting changes, novel objects, altered initial states, and reworded instructions. Hidden-state-based risk probes combined with functional conformal prediction can detect rollout failures, but their reliability depends on calibration data matching deployment conditions. We introduce SAFECAST, which leverages contrast set perturbations to improve hidden-state probe training and calibration for deployment time shift. SAFECAST statistically significantly improves failure detection ROC-AUC scores over a state of the art baseline in both real-world DROID and LIBERO simulation experiments across multiple VLM backbones. We further find that SAFECAST benefits most when both visual and language contrast set perturbations are used to augment data, and that with contrast set perturbations, sim-to-real calibration leads to better probes than using real rollout data only.

</details>


### [189] [Structured LLM Reasoning for Zero-Shot Human--Robot Coordination Under Hidden Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04309)
*Dong Hae Mangalindan,Anand Gokhale,Francesco Bullo,Vaibhav Srivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结构化的LLM架构，用于具有私有目标视图的合作构建任务中的零样本人机协调。基于Dec-POMDP框架，将决策分解为五个模块，通过人类实验证明该方法比无ToM消融和离线MARL基线更高效且获得更高信任评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有私有目标视图的合作构建任务中，人机协调面临信息不对称和复杂推理的挑战。传统方法（如多智能体强化学习）需要大量离线训练且泛化能力有限，而直接使用LLM进行端到端决策又缺乏结构和可靠性保障，因此需要一种系统化分解决策问题的方法。

Method: 基于Dec-POMDP公式化指导，构建结构化LLM架构，将决策分解为五个组件：(i) 基于动作条件的心理理论（Theory-of-Mind）推断，(ii) 层次化规划，(iii) 对话理解，(iv) 动作验证，以及(v) 基于反馈的重新规划。LLM作为难以处理的推断和规划计算的可行替代，同时保留传统验证手段确保物理可行性。

Result: 在人类参与者实验中，与无ToM推断的消融版本和离线训练的MARL策略相比，所提出的方法需要更少的交互步骤，并获得更高的交互后信任评分。

Conclusion: 系统化地分解团队决策问题，利用LLM作为不可解推理和规划计算的可行代理，并保留传统验证机制以确保物理可行性，可以同时改善任务协调效率和人类体验。

Abstract: We present a structured large-language-model (LLM) architecture for zero-shot human--robot coordination in a cooperative construction task with private goal views. Guided by a Dec-POMDP formulation, the architecture decomposes decision-making into (i) action-conditioned Theory-of-Mind (ToM) inference, (ii) hierarchical planning, (iii) conversation interpretation, (iv) action verification, and (v) feedback-based replanning. We compare the proposed method with an ablation without ToM inference and a multi-agent reinforcement-learning policy trained offline over many goal pairs. In human-participant experiments, the proposed method required fewer interaction steps and yielded higher post-interaction trust ratings than both baselines. These results suggest that systematically decomposing the team decision problem, using LLMs as tractable surrogates for otherwise intractable inference and planning computations, and retaining conventional verification for physical feasibility can improve both task coordination and the human experience.

</details>


### [190] [Design and Flight of an Ion-propelled Micro Hovercraft Leveraging Ground Proximity Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04343)
*C. Luke Nelson,Grant Nations,Mrinmoy Modak,Daniel S. Drew*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次展示了离子推进的微型气垫飞行器（hovercraft）。利用靠近地面时的电空气动力学（electroaerodynamic）地面效应，设计了一个手掌大小的气垫飞行器，该飞行器虽需外部电源供电，但可长时间飞行、承受多次起降循环、被动稳定抗扰动，且几乎零噪音。其推力效率达16 mN/W，额外有效载荷约1.5克（自身质量约1.6克），比同类离子推进机器人高出一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电空气动力学推进具有静音和固态特性，非常适合微型飞行器，但其效率受限，一直无法实现功率自主飞行。近期研究表明，在贴近地面运行时，小尺度大气离子推进器的推力密度和效率可大幅提升。作者希望探索利用这一地面效应设计厘米级气垫飞行器的设计空间，以突破效率瓶颈。

Method: 首先进行实验研究，对不同几何形状和配置的被动气垫裙边（hovercraft skirts）进行性能表征，分析其性能优势与权衡；随后基于实验结果制造一个可行的点设计方案（point design）。该设计为一个手掌大小的气垫飞行器，通过外部电源供电进行飞行测试。

Result: 成功演示了一个手掌大小的气垫飞行器：可持续飞行、承受数十次起降循环、被动稳定以抵御显著机械扰动、产生近乎为零的可听噪音。测得推力效率为16 mN/W，额外有效载荷约1.5克（自身质量约1.6克），性能超过同等尺寸电空气动力推进机器人一个数量级。

Conclusion: 这是公开文献中首次展示离子推进的微型气垫飞行器，证明利用地面效应的电空气动力学推进可以构建真正的飞行器，为全新一类机器人指明了方向。

Abstract: Electroaerodynamic propulsion is compelling for use in micro air vehicles due to its silent and solid-state nature, but its limited efficiency has thus far precluded a path towards power-autonomous flight. Recent work has shown that thrust density and efficiency for small-scale atmospheric ion thrusters can be vastly increased when operating close to a ground plane. Here, we explore the design space of centimeter-scale hovercraft, which can leverage this ground effect for low-altitude flight. We first perform an empirical investigation, characterizing the performance benefits and trade-offs for different geometries and configurations of passive hovercraft skirts, then use the results to fabricate a viable point design. We demonstrate a palm-sized hovercraft that, while tethered to an external power source, can fly for extended periods, withstand dozens of takeoff and landing cycles, passively stabilize to reject significant mechanical disturbances, and generate practically zero audible noise signature. The measured thrust efficiency of 16 mN/W and additional payload capacity of almost 1.5 grams above the vehicle's self mass of about 1.6 grams exceeds any similarly sized electroaerodynamically propelled robot by an order of magnitude. This is the first time an ion-propelled micro hovercraft has been shown in the open literature, and our work points the way towards an entirely new class of robot.

</details>


### [191] [RORA: Realistic Object Reconstruction with Articulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04842)
*Hyesung Lee,Youngseon Lee,Kyutae Lee,Dongjun Lee,Yongseok Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个端到端管线，通过基于建议的人工在环流程，从单段静态物体视频输入重建支持精确关节运动的仿真资产，结合3DGS与mesh混合表示，支持机器人学习中的实时灵巧操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有利用NeRF和3DGS将真实环境复现到仿真的方法在物体关节化（articulation）方面仍具挑战：现有运动跟踪或学习方法在含多关节的复杂运动学结构上成功率低，且需要扫描物体动态运动过程，使重建流程复杂化。

Method: 提出端到端管线，从单段静态物体视频输入出发，通过人工在环流程：1) 采用3DGS用于照片级渲染与mesh用于物理交互的混合表示；2) 进行凸分解并结合用户分组实现直观的部件分割；3) 将3D高斯绑定到对应mesh部件；4) 提出自动关节建议算法（Automatic Joint Suggestion Algorithm），从局部边界几何计算候选关节轴并呈现给用户，实现高效关节化资产重建。

Result: 在PartNet-Mobility-v0数据集和真实物体上实现了精确的关节化重建结果；展示了在Unreal Engine和NVIDIA Isaac Sim中部署重建资产，实现实时灵巧手操作任务的潜在应用。

Conclusion: 该方法通过人工在环的关节建议机制，实现了从单段静态视频到仿真就绪关节化资产的端到端重建，有效降低真实到仿真（real-to-sim）的差距，并验证了在机器人学习中的实用价值。

Abstract: Replicating real-world environments into simulation by realistic visual representation like NeRF and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as an effective strategy to reduce the sim-to-real gap in robot learning. However, implementing object articulation during the real-to-sim process is still a challenging task. Existing motion tracking or learning based articulation methods shows low success rates on complex kinematic structures having multiple joints. Furthermore, those methods require scan of dynamic motion of objects, which makes reconstruction process much complicated. In this work, we propose the first end-to-end pipeline that reconstructs simulation-ready assets with accurate articulation from a single static object video input through suggestion based human-in-the-loop process. Our approach exports a hybrid representation combining 3DGS for photorealistic rendering and mesh-based geometry for physical interaction. In the reconstruction process, our pipeline performs convex decomposition followed by user grouping for intuitive part segmentation, subsequently binding 3D Gaussians to the corresponding mesh parts. An Automatic Joint Suggestion Algorithm then calculates candidate joint axes from local boundary geometries and presents them to users for efficient articulated asset reconstruction. We have shown that our method achieves precise articulation results on partnet-mobility-v0 dataset and real objects. Additionally we presented a potential usage of our framework on robot learning, deploying the reconstructed assets in Unreal Engine and NVIDIA Isaac Sim, demonstrating real-time dexterous hand manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [192] [Approximate Multi-Objective Search Under Rulebooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04398)
*Omar Muhammetkulyyev,Oren Salzman,Tichakorn Wongpiromsarn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RA*pex算法，一种基于ε-rule-dominance概念的最优优先搜索算法，能够高效计算ε-近似规则簿最优解集的紧凑集合，计算速度比现有方法快两个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人规划常涉及具有复杂优先级关系的多目标优化（如安全、效率、合规性）。规则簿（rulebooks）通过偏序关系形式化这些优先级，但计算完整的规则簿最优解集计算开销巨大，需要一种高效的近似求解方法。

Method: 引入ε-rule-dominance（ε-规则支配）概念作为规则簿下近似支配的规范化定义，提出RA*pex最佳优先搜索算法。算法利用降维技术加速多目标搜索，同时通过维护分离的闭合集（closed sets）并对截断规则集和残差规则集执行支配检查来尊重规则层次结构。

Result: 形式化证明了返回集合中至少有一个解ε-规则支配每个规则簿最优解。实验表明计算时间比现有方法快两个数量级以上。

Conclusion: RA*pex通过ε-近似概念和规则层次感知的搜索策略，在保证近似最优解覆盖的前提下显著提升计算效率，为大规模多目标机器人规划问题提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Robotic planning often involves multiple objectives with complex priority relationships, such as safety, efficiency, and regulatory compliance. Rulebooks formalize these relationships, allowing partial ordering of objectives that generalizes both Pareto and lexicographic dominance. Computing the full set of rulebook-optimal solutions, however, is computationally expensive. To address this challenge, we introduce the concept of epsilon-rule-dominance, a principled notion of approximate dominance under rulebooks, and propose RA*pex, a best-first search algorithm that efficiently computes a compact set of epsilon-approximate rulebook-optimal solutions. RA*pex leverages dimensionality reduction, a technique used to speed up existing multi-objective search algorithms, while respecting rule hierarchies by maintaining separate closed sets and performing dominance checks over truncated and residual rule sets. We provide a formal analysis of RA*pex, proving that every rulebook-optimal solution is epsilon-rule-dominated (a generalization of approximate dominance we introduce) by at least one solution in the returned set. Empirical results demonstrate that our approach achieves computation times over two orders of magnitude faster than existing methods.

</details>


### [193] [SCOPE: Field-of-View-Aware Path Planning in Unknown 3D Environments via Safety-Volume Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04420)
*Junbin Yuan,Muqing Cao,Yunwoo Lee,Brady Moon,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCOPE是一个面向未知环境的在线安全体积认证规划框架，通过构造认证图并解耦乐观引导与认证执行，实现了在有限视场传感器条件下的安全导航，并在60个随机任务及真实机器人实验中验证了其完整性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境中，使用机身安装的有限视场（limited field-of-view）传感器进行安全导航，要求在每次运动执行前，机器人的完整膨胀体积所经过的空间都已被观测并确认无障碍。这一严格的在线安全认证需求给传统规划方法带来了挑战，需要在未知体素地图中实时验证安全性。

Method: 构建认证图（certified graph），其顶点对应具有完全已知空闲安全体积的位置；提出SCOPE框架，将乐观的目标引导与认证执行解耦；将乐观路径上第一个未认证点转化为显式观测义务，通过目标中心的视角搜索解决，并在有用视角尚未认证可达时递归清除中间义务；结合认证预览机制和考虑观测的轨迹优化后端实现平滑执行。

Result: 在三个未知3D环境中的60个随机任务里，SCOPE均成功到达目标，同时保持近乎零比例地进入未认证膨胀空间；认证预览机制使平均任务时间减少27%；在两个代表性场景中的真实机器人演示验证了完整系统的可行性。

Conclusion: SCOPE在理想单调感知和穷举有限域图搜索的条件下，具备条件完全规划性质：只要在其规划原语内存在有限的可行认证感知动作序列，就能到达目标。该系统同时实现了安全性与完成性，并通过实验证明了其实际可用性。

Abstract: Safe navigation with a body-mounted limited-field-of-view sensor requires the complete robot-inflated volume of an intended motion to be observed and verified free before execution. We formulate this requirement as online safety-volume certification in an unknown voxel map and construct a certified graph whose vertices correspond exactly to positions with fully known-free safety volumes. Based on this representation, we propose SCOPE (Safety Certification through Observation Planning and Execution), a planning framework that decouples optimistic goal-directed guidance from certified execution. SCOPE converts the first uncertified point along an optimistic route into an explicit observation obligation, resolves it through target-centric viewpoint search, and recursively clears intermediate obligations when useful viewpoints are not yet certified-reachable. A certified preview mechanism and an observation-aware trajectory optimization backend enable smooth execution. We prove conditional complete planning: under ideal monotone sensing and exhaustive finite-domain graph search, SCOPE reaches the goal whenever a finite feasible sequence of certified sensing actions exists within its planning primitives. Across 60 randomized tasks in three unknown 3D environments, SCOPE reaches every goal while maintaining near-zero entry into non-certified inflated space. Preview reduces mean mission time by 27%, and real-robot demonstrations in two representative scenarios validate the complete system.

</details>


### [194] [SSC: A Verifiable Structured Representation for Bimanual Manipulation Labelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04425)
*Yupu Lu,Shuang Wu,Sihan Chen,Ruihua Han,Yichen Zhang,Marcus Kalander,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化子任务链（SSC），一种介于自由自然语言与刚性模板之间的状态转换表示方法，用于长时程操作演示的子任务标注。SSC基于结构化子任务模板（SST），支持自然语言渲染、四规则逻辑校验和查询解析补全三条视觉-语言辅助功能，并在BEHAVIOR-1K上实例化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子任务标签存在两难困境：自然语言描述可读性好但语言变异性大、难以自动验证；而刚性模板（如BEHAVIOR-1K的skill_annotation）语言上过度切分，损害可读性和标注一致性。需要一种介于两者之间的表示形式，兼顾可读性、逻辑可验证性和标注一致性。

Method: 提出结构化子任务链（SSC）。演示被表示为一系列结构化子任务模板（SST）条目，每个SST包含：核心动作组件（主语、谓语、宾语）、灵活条件（空间或工具类状语修饰语）、与手臂动作分离的基础运动字段，以及后置状态场景图。SSC支持三个视觉-语言辅助功能：(1)将SST渲染为自然语言；(2)依据四条状态转换规则检查链条逻辑；(3)通过查询解析级联补全未定义字段。在BEHAVIOR-1K（50个任务、每任务3个episode、2,357个标注动作单元）上实例化流水线进行逻辑验证和内容补全，评估13个SOTA视觉-语言模型作为候选验证器并报告标注异常。

Result: 在BEHAVIOR-1K数据集上完成了SSC流水线的实例化，对2,357个标注动作单元进行了逻辑验证和内容补全，评估了13个选定的SOTA视觉语言模型作为候选验证器并报告了标注异常情况。

Conclusion: SSC作为一种结构化子任务链表示，在自由文本与刚性模板之间取得了平衡，通过状态转换规则和查询解析机制提升了长时程操作演示子任务标注的可验证性、一致性和可读性。

Abstract: Subtask labels decompose a long-horizon manipulation demonstration into shorter semantic segments for policy training and evaluation. Natural language descriptions are easy to read, but their linguistic variability makes automatic verification difficult. Rigid template formats, such as BEHAVIOR-1K's skill_annotation, are linguistically over-segmented, hindering both readability and annotation consistency. We propose the Structured Subtask Chain (SSC), a state-transition representation that bridges these extremes. A demonstration is a sequence of Structured Subtask Template (SST) entries. Each SST stores core action components (subject, predicate, object), flexible conditions (adverbial modifiers such as spatial or instrumental phrases), a base-motion field separate from arm actions, and an after-state scene graph. Built on this format, SSC supports three vision-language assisted functions: rendering SSTs as natural language, checking the assembled chain against four state-transition rules, and completing underspecified fields through a query resolution cascade. We instantiate the pipeline on BEHAVIOR-1K (50 tasks, 3 episodes per task, 2,357 annotated action cells) for logic verification and content completion, evaluating 13 selected state-of-the-art VL models as candidate verifiers and reporting labelling anomalies.

</details>


### [195] [GUARD: Grounding Uncertainty and Ablation-Based Risk Detection for Diffusion-Based VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04510)
*Suhas Hegde,Jitendra Yasaswi Bharadwaj Katta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GUARD是一种基于扩散的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的测试时故障检测方法，通过测量动作预测对多模态证据的依赖程度来检测未充分对齐的任务失败，无需修改预训练策略，在五个基准设置中取得了最优的未见任务ROC-AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式VLA策略即使其预测与定义任务的视觉和语言证据关系微弱时，仍可能生成看似合理的动作，导致任务失败难以检测。现有方法缺乏对这种"弱grounding"问题的有效运行时监控手段。

Method: GUARD通过估计最终VLM键值（KV）缓存中token索引条目的影响，构造反事实缓存（消融显著KV条目），并与原始条件去噪响应进行比较。基于比较结果推导出包含敏感性、注意力熵、模态偏差和grounding效率的诊断流，这些指标在线校准后由轻量级时序分类器处理。

Result: 在五个策略基准设置中使用Pi0、SmolVLA和Alpamayo-1.5评估（LIBERO、SimplerEnv、MetaWorld、PhysicalAI-AV），GUARD在五个未见任务设置中的四个达到最优ROC-AUC，在剩余一个上排名第二。未见任务平均ROC-AUC比最强竞争运行时监控器提升5.73个百分点，同时与最佳已见任务平均仅相差0.19个百分点。

Conclusion: 直接探测动作头对多模态证据的依赖性，提供了一种跨策略、任务、具身和领域的可迁移失败信号，能够在不修改预训练策略的前提下有效检测行动与视觉/语言证据之间的失配。

Abstract: Diffusion-based vision-language-action (VLA) policies can generate plausible actions even when their predictions are weakly grounded in the visual and language evidence defining the task. We introduce GUARD, a test-time failure detection method that measures this grounding without modifying the pretrained policy. GUARD estimates the influence of token-indexed entries in the final vision-language model key-value (KV) cache, constructs counterfactual caches by ablating salient KV entries, and compares their denoising responses with the original conditioning. Based on the comparison, we derive GUARD diagnostic stream including sensitivity, attention entropy, modality bias, and grounding efficiency, which are calibrated online and processed by a lightweight temporal classifier. We evaluate GUARD under task-held-out splits across five policy-benchmark settings, using Pi0, SmolVLA, and Alpamayo-1.5 on LIBERO, SimplerEnv, MetaWorld, and PhysicalAI-AV. GUARD achieves the best ROC-AUC on four of five unseen-task settings and ranks second on the remaining setting, improving the average unseen-task ROC-AUC by 5.73 percentage points over the strongest competing runtime monitor while remaining within 0.19 points of the best seen-task average. These results show that directly probing action-head dependence on multimodal evidence provides a transferable failure signal across policies, tasks, embodiments, and domains.

</details>


### [196] [Retrieve in Time, Correct in Frequency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04527)
*Yuze Fan,Yue Cao,Pengjie Gao,Haojia Gao,Guangqiu Guo,Ziyue Zhang,Junbo Tan,Bokui Chen,Zhuo Zou,Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RTCF框架，一种无需训练的测试时校正方法，通过渐进式记忆对齐检索时间对齐的成功经验，并从频率域提取裁剪后的低频残差来校正冻结的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，在不更新参数、不增加推理或GPU开销的情况下，将LIBERO基准集成功率从86.4%提升至88.4%，长时程任务从61.6%提升至68.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的VLA策略生成时间上扩展的动作块，但长时程操作易受累积执行误差和跨任务阶段的视觉混淆影响。成功轨迹提供了有用的纠错证据，但现有的帧检索可能返回进度错位的动作，直接回放或时域融合又会覆盖策略提案的反应式结构，因此需要一种既能把时间对齐做到位、又不破坏策略响应特性的校正方法。

Method: RTCF将"检索什么经验"与"迁移动作的哪部分"解耦。具体包括：(1) 渐进式记忆对齐（PMA），通过增量更新的单调前沿将不断增长的视觉执行历史与完整成功轨迹进行因果对齐，无需阶段标签即可联合识别相关记忆与当前对齐位置；(2) 从对齐的动作块中，仅在运动通道上迁移系数级裁剪的低频残差，而高频成分与夹爪决策保留来自冻结策略的原始输出。整个流程在单次策略调用后于客户端CPU执行，无需参数更新或额外GPU资源。

Result: 在四个LIBERO任务套件、每个条件下2,000个回合的实验中，RTCF将聚合成功率从86.4%提升至88.4%，其中LIBERO-Long从61.6%提升至68.6%。校正可在客户端CPU上完成，每个动作块的校正延迟中位数总计仅10.99毫秒。

Conclusion: RTCF证明了一种训练无关、可低成本部署的测试时校正方案有效：通过从成功经验中提取时间对齐的低频运动残差来校正冻结VLA策略，可以在显著提升长时程任务成功率的同时，几乎不引入模型侧开销，为现有VLA策略的即插即用改进提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Frozen vision-language-action (VLA) policies generate temporally extended action chunks, but long-horizon manipulation remains vulnerable to accumulated execution error and visual aliasing across task stages. Successful rollouts provide useful corrective evidence, yet current frame retrieval can return progress-misaligned actions,while direct replay or time-domain fusion can overwrite the reactive structure of the policy proposal. We introduce Retrieve in Time, Correct in Frequency (RTCF), a training-free test-time correction framework that improves frozen VLA performance with low model-side overhead.RTCF separates which experience to retrieve from which part of its action to transfer. Progressive Memory Alignment (PMA) causally aligns the growing visual execution history with complete successful trajectories through incrementally updated monotonic frontiers, jointly identifying a relevant memory and the current aligned memory position without stage labels. From the aligned action chunk,RTCF transfers a coefficient-wise-clipped low-frequency residual on motion channels. Higher-frequency components and gripper decisions remain inherited from the frozen policy. Across four LIBERO suites and 2,000 episodes per condition, RTCF raises aggregate success from 86.4% to 88.4% and improves LIBERO-Long from 61.6% to 68.6%.These gains require no parameter updates, repeated VLA inference, or additional GPU resources: correction can be performed on the client CPU after a single policy invocation, and the median latencies sum to only 10.99 ms per action chunk

</details>


### [197] [Static Timing Orchestration for Tree-Structured Robot Control Firmware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04600)
*Wang Xi,Feiran Wei,Mo Deng,Weiheng Lin,Pangkit Fong,Jianping He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FineMote，一个面向树状结构设备模型的控制固件生成框架，利用编译期信息静态确定执行顺序以满足截止时间和前驱约束，并证明其决策延迟上界，实验验证了更好的时序表现和运行时响应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人系统日益复杂，从结构描述文件生成控制固件成为降低开发复杂度和提升可维护性的有效范式。然而，现有机器人描述格式天然表示为树形层次结构，这种结构引入了结构化的数据依赖，影响着感知到决策的延迟，进而影响控制性能。

Method: 提出FineMote框架，包含：(1) 将异构低层控制逻辑对象化，暴露统一的调度单元和执行入口；(2) 基于对象层次结构的调度机制，利用编译期信息静态确定执行顺序，最小化运行时开销；(3) 形式化证明该机制满足截止时间和前驱约束，并推导出树内决策延迟的上界。

Result: 在真实机器人控制平台上进行实验评估，结果表明系统在时序行为和运行时响应性方面均有改善，验证了所提设计的实际有效性。

Conclusion: FineMote通过编译期静态调度策略有效应对树状结构设备模型带来的数据依赖问题，在保证截止时间和前驱约束的同时降低了决策延迟，为复杂机器人系统的固件自动生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As robotic systems become increasingly complex, generating control firmware from structural description files has emerged as a promising paradigm for reducing development complexity and improving maintainability. Existing robot description formats naturally represent robotic systems as hierarchical tree structures, where devices are recursively composed into functional subsystems and eventually into the complete robot. However, such tree-structured organization also introduces structured data dependencies that affect perception-to-decision latency and, consequently, control performance.
  In this paper, we propose FineMote, a control firmware generation framework with a scheduling mechanism tailored for tree-structured device models. The framework objectifies heterogeneous low-level control logic and exposes unified scheduling units and execution entry points. Based on the resulting object hierarchy, the scheduling mechanism exploits compile-time information to statically determine execution order with minimal runtime overhead. We prove that the proposed mechanism satisfies deadline and precedence constraints, and further derive an upper bound on intra-tree decision latency. We implement the proposed framework and evaluate it on real robotic control platforms. The experimental results show improved timing behavior and runtime responsiveness, demonstrating the practical effectiveness of the proposed design.

</details>


### [198] [GASP: GPU-Accelerated Safe Planner for Real-Time Collision-Aware Motion Generation with Latent Trajectory Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04612)
*Colin Merk,Stefanos Charalambous,Peter Dürr,Farshad Khadivar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GASP，一个GPU加速的安全运动规划器，结合钳位B样条参数化和卷积残差神经网络，在已知环境中实现实时、碰撞感知的关节空间运动生成，推理时间接近毫秒级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已知环境中实时生成碰撞感知的关节空间运动轨迹是一个关键挑战。传统轨迹优化方法推理时间较长，难以满足实时性要求，需要一种兼顾安全性与效率的快速规划方法。

Method: GASP采用钳位B样条表示轨迹，通过卷积残差神经网络预测内部的自由控制点，并在边界处解析插入控制点以强制执行初始和最终导数约束。使用条件变分自编码器采样多个轨迹候选，并在GPU上并行解码和验证，形成批量规划器。

Result: 作为在线运动生成模块，GASP达到了与分析级方法相当的成功率，具有很高的碰撞感知可行性，并相对基于GPU的轨迹优化显著减少了推理时间。在竞技机器人乒乓球中作为强化学习重置规划器，匹配基线回球率的同时将训练期碰撞减少约一半。

Conclusion: GASP证明了GPU加速的神经网络规划方法能够在保证碰撞安全性前提下实现近毫秒级的实时关节空间运动生成，并可有效嵌入强化学习训练流程中提升训练效率。

Abstract: We present GASP, a GPU-Accelerated Safe Planner for real-time, collision-aware joint-space motion generation in known environments. GASP combines a clamped B-spline trajectory parameterization with a convolutional residual neural network that predicts the free interior control points, while analytically inserted boundary control points enforce initial and final derivative constraints for collision-aware planning under non-stationary conditions. A conditional variational autoencoder samples multiple trajectory candidates, which are decoded and validated in parallel on the GPU, yielding a batched planner for collision-aware coupled joint-space motion with near-millisecond inference. We validate GASP as an online motion-generation module, where it achieves analytical-level success rates with high collision-aware feasibility and substantially reduces inference time relative to GPU-based trajectory optimization. We further deploy GASP as a reinforcement-learning reset planner in competitive robotic table tennis, matching the baseline return rate while roughly halving training-time collisions.

</details>


### [199] [Mind-VLA: Instruction-Aware Spatial Representation Alignment for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04633)
*Xingyu Ding,Yuzhong Zhao,Yang Wu,Chaoyang Zhao,Chunhai Zhao,Yifan Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Mind-VLA是一种指令感知的空间表示对齐方法，通过将VLA模型的潜在表示与语言指令指定的目标物体的三视图VAE和VGGT特征对齐，实现细粒度操控和目标遮挡场景下的3D理解。在LIBERO上达到93.9%，在CALVIN上达到4.47，在真实机器人目标遮挡任务上平均成功率54%，比最佳指令无关方法高出32个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA方法通过对齐3D场景几何来提升泛化能力，但这些方法本质上是指令无关的——它们将整个场景均匀对齐，忽视了语言指令指定的具体目标物体的3D几何。这导致在细粒度操控和目标遮挡等依赖目标物体精确3D理解的场景中表现不佳。

Method: Mind-VLA首先通过语言指令获取目标物体，然后准备目标物体的三视图（tri-view）并提取对应的VAE和VGGT特征，最后将VLA模型的潜在表示与这些特征对齐，以实现指令感知的3D理解。

Result: Mind-VLA在LIBERO上达到93.9%，在CALVIN上达到4.47（使用紧凑的345M参数骨干网络）。在真实机器人目标遮挡任务中达到54%的平均成功率，比最佳指令无关方法高出32个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过将注意力从整个场景转向指令指定的目标物体，指令感知的空间表示对齐能够显著提升VLA模型在细粒度操控和遮挡场景下的性能，代码将公开。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) methods improve generalization by aligning their representations with 3D scene geometry. However, these methods are fundamentally instruction-agnostic: the representations align the entire scene uniformly, neglecting the 3D geometry of the specific target object designated by the language instruction. This causes failures on fine-grained manipulation and target occlusion tasks, where success depends on accurate 3D understanding of the target object rather than the entire scene. To address this, we present Mind-VLA, an instruction-aware spatial representation alignment method for VLA models. Specifically, Mind-VLA first obtains the target object specified by the language instruction, then prepares its target-object tri-view and extracts the corresponding VAE and VGGT features. Finally, the latent representation of the VLA model is aligned with these features to enable instruction-aware 3D understanding. Mind-VLA reaches 93.9% on LIBERO and 4.47 on CALVIN with a compact 345M-parameter backbone. On real-robot tasks with target occlusion, Mind-VLA reaches 54% average success, outperforming the best-performing instruction-agnostic method in real-robot comparison by 32 percentage points. Code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [200] [Suppression Sticks, Locality Is Fragile: A Closed-Loop Target-and-Control Audit of Task-Vector Negation in VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04692)
*Shaoguang Wang,Weiyu Guo,Rushi Dai,Yiren Zhao,Yandong Guo,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对多任务视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略中的逐技能任务向量减法进行了目标与控制审计，发现其在闭环机器人控制中的行为局部性较差：10个LIBERO-Goal技能中5个可实现目标抑制但控制保留率仅52%，其余技能表现为抵抗或全局崩溃，且对标称无关控制的伤害普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务向量运算提供了一种闭式（closed-form）的模型编辑方法，但其在闭环机器人控制情境下的行为局部性仍不清楚。研究者希望通过系统的目标-控制审计来评估该方法在体现式模型编辑中的可靠性和适用边界。

Method: 对多任务VLA策略执行逐技能任务向量减法，在LIBERO-Goal的10个技能上进行全量审计；扩展到不同策略（连续回归、离散token、流匹配动作头）验证跨策略稳健性；在Spatial、Object和Long-horizon面板上进行对照；采用匹配范数控制分析符号不对称性，引入保留感知的梯度基线作为比较器，并通过单技能重学习探针检验行为掩蔽。

Result: 减法产生三种定性不同的行为状态：5个技能出现目标-控制分离，3个为抵抗，2个为全局崩溃。可抑制目标的保持集成功率仍为0%，但平均基线归一化控制保留率仅52%，每个目标抑制编辑至少显著损害一个标称无关控制。任务向量余弦均值无法解释这种变异性，多向量结果随锚点和尺度变化。

Conclusion: 任务向量减法是一种快速但脆弱的干预手段，其行为局部性在闭环机器人控制中不成立；评估体现式模型编辑的局部性时必须进行闭环目标-控制综合评价，而非仅依赖任务向量相似度等静态指标。

Abstract: Task-vector arithmetic offers a closed-form way to modify a model, yet its behavioral locality remains unclear in closed-loop robot control. We present a target-and-control audit of per-skill task-vector subtraction from multitask vision-language-action (VLA) policies. Across all ten LIBERO-Goal skills, subtraction produces three qualitatively different regimes: target-control separation for five skills, resistance for three, and global collapse for two. On held-out initial states, the five suppressible targets remain at 0% success; however, mean baseline-normalized control retention is only 52%, and each target-suppressing edit materially harms at least one nominally unrelated control. Additional Goal panels show separation across tested policies with continuous-regression, discrete-token, and flow-matching action heads, whereas we observe no clean separation on Spatial and control collapse on the tested Object and Long-horizon panels. Mean task-vector cosine does not account for this variation. A matched-norm control identifies a local sign asymmetry around one Goal anchor, while multi-vector outcomes vary with anchor and scale. Retain-aware gradient baselines provide data-dependent comparators but require removal-time data and optimization; subtraction is data- and gradient-free only at edit time, assuming precomputed expert deltas. Finally, a single-skill relearning probe is consistent with behavioral masking, not certified unlearning. These results characterize task-vector subtraction as a fast but brittle intervention and underscore the need for closed-loop target-and-control evaluation when assessing locality in embodied model editing.

</details>


### [201] [Enabling Urgency-aware Robot Swarm Intralogistics using Smart IoT Tags](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04721)
*Youssef Alboraei,Murray Groves,Shane Wen,Wenda Zhao,Senhui Qiu,Mohammud J. Bocus,Robert Piechocki,Sabine Hauert,Kerstin Eder*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种去中心化机器人群体仓储物流系统，每个仓库载体携带超低功耗IoT标签，通过蓝牙低功耗（BLE）广播其物品的紧急程度。机器人直接读取这些广播，在选择服务哪个载体时权衡紧急程度与行驶距离，从而在没有中央调度的情况下实现物品级别的优先级分配。仿真和实物实验表明该系统能显著提高紧急物品的优先服务比例，同时保持较低的延迟和吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓储物品在搬运紧急程度上存在差异（如易腐货物、药品运输、JIT生产材料需更快交付），但现有去中心化群体控制器要么对所有物品一视同仁，要么依赖外部调度器设置优先级，导致紧急物品与普通物品等待时间相同。需要一种无需固定自动化基础设施、又能实现物品级优先级分配的去中心化方案。

Method: （1）每个仓库载体携带超低功耗IoT标签，通过BLE广播其物品的紧急程度；（2）机器人在选择搬运哪个载体时，直接读取这些广播，并将紧急程度与行驶距离进行权衡决策；（3）优先级分配发生在物品层面，无需中央调度器；（4）在仿真和真实机器人+IoT标记载体上进行评估，与仅基于接近度的基线系统对比。

Result: 实物实验中，优先对齐率（紧急物品先被服务的比例）从0.41提升到0.64，P95交付延迟呈非显著下降趋势，吞吐量保持在基线的1.2%以内。仿真中收益随系统规模增大：在三种更大规模配置下，P95延迟降低5.2%-11.8%，优先对齐率提升41.7%-51.6%。

Conclusion: 将紧急程度附着于物品本身，可使去中心化机器人群体优先服务时间敏感的库存，同时保持群体系统低基础设施要求的优点，使其在仓储自动化中具有吸引力。

Abstract: Warehouse items differ in how urgently they must be moved: perishable goods, pharmaceutical shipments, and just-in-time production materials must be delivered sooner than the rest of the stock.
