Decentralised robot swarms suit warehouses that cannot justify fixed automation infrastructure, but current swarm controllers treat all items alike or rely on an external scheduler to set priorities, so urgent items wait as long as ordinary ones. This paper presents a swarm logistics system in which each warehouse carrier holds an ultra-low-power Internet-of-Things (IoT) tag that broadcasts the urgency of its item over Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE). Robots read these broadcasts directly and weigh urgency against travel distance when choosing which carrier to serve, so prioritisation happens at the item level without central scheduling. The system is evaluated in simulation and validated on real robots and IoT-tagged carriers against a proximity-only baseline. In the physical trials, priority alignment (i.e. proportion of urgent items served first), improved from 0.41 to 0.64, with a nonsignificant trend toward lower 95th-percentile (P95) delivery latency and throughput within 1.2% of the baseline. In simulation, the benefit grew with system size: across three larger configurations, P95 latency fell by 5.2% to 11.8% and priority alignment improved by 41.7% to 51.6%. Attaching urgency to the items themselves therefore allows a decentralised swarm to serve time-critical stock sooner while keeping the low infrastructure requirements that make swarm systems attractive for warehouse automation.

</details>


### [202] [A Vision-based Control Framework for Real-time Autonomous UUV Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04723)
*Erik Tjærand Frøland,Marco Job,Md Ether Deowan,Eleni Kelasidi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个全集成、基于视觉的框架，用于水下无人航行器（UUV）在动态、视觉挑战环境中的实时鲁棒定位、自主导航与建图。该框架支持网相对定位和全局定位，并实时生成连续3D地图，在合成数据集和实际UUV自主导航实验中均验证了实时性能和增强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境动态且视觉条件复杂（能见度低、光照变化、散射等），传统定位导航方法面临巨大挑战，需要一种既能实时又能鲁棒工作的视觉驱动方案，以支撑UUV在水下关键检测与建图任务中的自主部署。

Method: 提出一个完全集成的视觉框架，采用统一流水线同时实现两种定位模式：（1）网相对定位（net-relative localization），用于相对参考网的自主导航；（2）全局定位，用于更广范围的环境理解。系统在导航过程中实时连续生成周围环境的3D地图。

Result: 在带ground truth的合成数据集上完成验证，并在实际UUV上进行了自主网相对导航实验。结果表明系统达到实时性能，并在动态与视觉退化环境中展现出增强的鲁棒性，支持视觉驱动的自主导航。

Conclusion: 该框架为UUV在实际复杂水下环境中的自主导航和3D建图提供了可行的视觉解决方案，推动了海洋机器人在关键检测与建图任务中的现场部署能力。

Abstract: This paper presents a fully integrated vision-based framework for real-time and robust localization, autonomous navigation, and mapping for unmanned underwater vehicles (UUVs) in dynamic, visually challenging environments. The proposed pipeline enables both net-relative and global localization while generating continuous 3D maps of the surroundings in real-time. The framework was validated on synthetic datasets with ground truth and tested onboard an UUV during autonomous net-relative navigation experiments. Results demonstrate real-time performance and enhanced robustness, supporting vision-driven autonomous navigation and enabling the field deployment of marine robots for critical inspection and mapping tasks in complex underwater environments.

</details>


### [203] [A GitOps-Driven Annotation Catalog for Fully Automatic Railway Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04724)
*Martin Köppel,Tobias Cronauer,Zekiye Ilknur-Öz,Sebastian Dubiel,Patrick Naumann,Philipp Neumaier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GitOps的轻量级元数据管理架构，用于自动驾驶列车（GoA3-GoA4）AI感知系统的数据集标注管理。该架构利用Data-as-Code、CI/CD流水线和静态站点生成技术，实现开发者友好的工作流、可追溯性、法规合规性和高性能数据集概览，解决了传统单体数据目录操作开销大、工作流集成差和文档漂移等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶列车（GoA3-GoA4）依赖AI感知系统，其性能高度取决于大规模、高质量、高动态的标注数据集。但现有的元数据管理面临三大挑战：管理元数据、维护来源追踪、跟踪标注的迭代演进取悦巨大的基础设施和法规要求；而现有的单体数据目录存在大量操作开销、开发者工作流集成差、文档严重漂移等问题。

Method: 提出一种基于GitOps的轻量级元数据管理架构，核心包括：1) Data-as-Code原则，将数据元数据作为代码管理；2) CI/CD流水线，实现自动化校验和部署；3) 静态站点生成（SSG），自动生成高性能的数据集概览页面。通过这套架构建立无缝、开发者中心化的工作流。

Result: 该架构实现了完整的数据可追溯性，执行严格的法规合规性要求，并自动生成高性能的数据集概览。相比传统单体数据目录，显著降低了操作开销并消除了文档漂移问题。

Conclusion: 本文提出的GitOps元数据管理架构为轨道交通AI感知领域的标注数据管理提供了轻量化、开发者友好的替代方案，将工程实践（CI/CD、Data-as-Code）与自动化文档生成相结合，兼顾了可追溯性、合规性和性能需求。

Abstract: Automatic train operation (ATO) at grade of automation 3 and above (GoA3-GoA4) requires robust AI-based perception systems capable of reliably detecting obstacles and railway-specific objects under real-world conditions. The effectiveness of these modern artificial intelligence approaches depends heavily on large-scale, high-quality, and highly dynamic annotated datasets. However, managing metadata, maintaining provenance, and tracking the iterative evolution of these annotations impose significant infrastructural and regulatory requirements. Existing monolithic data catalogs often suffer from massive operational overhead, poor integration into developer workflows, and severe documentation drift. This paper introduces an innovative, lightweight GitOps-based architecture for metadata management. By leveraging Data-as-Code principles, Continuous Integration/Continuous Deployment (CI/CD) pipelines, and Static Site Generation (SSG), the proposed approach establishes a seamless, developer-centric workflow. This ensures an traceability, enforces strict regulatory compliance, and automatically generates a highly performant dataset overview.

</details>


### [204] [Explicit Language Memory for Long-Horizon Planning in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04765)
*Houze Xu,Jizhong Li,Ziyi Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种带显式语言记忆模块的分层长时程VLA架构，将高层VLM的语义推理与低层VLA的连续控制解耦，通过递归更新的文本记忆序列实现时间一致性和闭环纠错，在仿真和真实机器人实验中验证了该方法在复杂长时程任务中提升成功率和鲁棒性，并提供可解释的决策语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在长时程任务中面临四大挑战：稀疏专家示范限制跨任务组合泛化、非马尔可夫性导致仅依赖当前观测难以保持时间一致性、闭环纠错能力有限导致误差累积、端到端动作微调可能削弱VLM骨干的高层语义表征。这些问题阻碍了VLA在真实长时程场景中的部署。

Method: 提出分层长时程VLA架构，包含显式语言记忆模块。核心思想是将离散时间观测转化为具有时间逻辑的连贯文本记忆序列。系统解耦为高层VLM和低层VLA：高层VLM通过视觉问答训练范式进行语义推理，递归更新语言记忆和子任务指令，以前一记忆作为上下文锚点；低层VLA基于子任务指令和视觉观测执行精确连续控制。

Result: 在多个仿真环境和真实机器人平台的sim-to-real实验中，显式语言记忆显著提升了VLA模型在复杂长时程任务中的成功率和鲁棒性，同时提供了可解释的决策过程语义描述。

Conclusion: 显式语言记忆模块通过将时间观测编码为文本记忆序列，有效解决了VLA长时程任务中的时间一致性和误差累积问题，验证了分层架构（高层语义推理+低层连续控制）在长时程机器人操控中的可行性和可解释性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide a unified paradigm for connecting visual perception, language understanding, and robotic control. However, existing VLA models still face major challenges in long-horizon tasks: sparse expert demonstrations constrain cross-task compositional generalization; the non-Markovian nature of long-horizon tasks makes it difficult for policies conditioned only on current observations to maintain temporal consistency; limited closed-loop error correction allows execution errors to accumulate; and end-to-end action fine-tuning may weaken the high-level semantic representations of vision-language model (VLM) backbones. To address these issues, we propose a hierarchical long-horizon VLA architecture with an explicit language-memory module. The central idea is to convert discrete temporal observations into a coherent textual memory sequence with temporal logic. The system is decoupled into a high-level VLM and a low-level VLA: the high-level VLM performs semantic reasoning through a visual question answering training paradigm, while the low-level VLA executes precise continuous control conditioned on subtask instructions and visual observations. The high-level VLM recursively updates both language memory and subtask instructions using the previous memory as a contextual anchor, enabling persistent temporal tracking and dynamic correction during long-horizon execution. We evaluate the proposed method in multiple simulation environments and conduct sim-to-real experiments on a real robotic platform. The results demonstrate that explicit language memory improves the success rate and robustness of VLA models on complex long-horizon tasks while providing an interpretable semantic account of the decision process.

</details>


### [205] [From Transparent Labware Segmentation to Collision Avoidance: A Real-Time Edge-Aware Perception Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04769)
*Shijun Ding,Chen Qian,Weiwei Shang,Junlin Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种边缘感知的实例分割框架，利用透明玻璃器皿的边界轮廓作为可靠视觉线索，实现纯视觉驱动的实时机器人防碰撞。方法包括边缘检测分支、边缘引导注意力融合和SimAM模块，并构建了LabGlass-IS数据集（3485张图像、21类）。模型在Boundary F-score上达到97.80，推理速度7.1ms/帧，参数仅为最接近精度竞争者的2.85%，真实机器人实验中碰撞避免成功率达93.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明玻璃器皿因折射、镜面反射和缺乏稳定内部纹理，难以通过常规分割方法处理，但它们的边界轮廓相对可靠。现有方法无法在实时性和精度上同时满足机器人在脆弱透明物体间防碰撞的需求，亟需一种纯视觉、高效且准确的实例分割方案。

Method: 基于单阶段实时实例分割骨干网络，增设轻量级边缘检测分支，引入边缘引导注意力融合机制和无需参数的SimAM模块；构建LabGlass-IS数据集；通过多视角三角化掩码质心获得物体3D位置，并利用保守包围体约束实现碰撞规避。

Result: Boundary F-score达到97.80，比YOLO-prompted FastSAM高出18.93 BF点；推理速度7.1ms/帧；参数量仅为最接近精度竞争者的2.85%；真实机器人碰撞避免成功率达93.3%。

Conclusion: 实验表明该感知-动作流水线在透明易碎物体环境下的机器人防碰撞是可行的，为透明物体实例分割和机器人安全导航提供了高效的纯视觉解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents an edge-aware instance segmentation framework that enables real-time robotic collision avoidance with transparent laboratory glassware using purely visual perception. Transparent vessels defy conventional segmentation due to refraction, specular reflection, and the absence of stable interior texture, yet their boundary contours remain comparatively reliable visual cues. Exploiting this observation, we augment a one-stage real-time instance segmentation backbone with a lightweight edge-detection branch, edge-guided attention fusion, and a parameter-free SimAM module, and further construct LabGlass-IS, a 3485-image, 21-category instance segmentation dataset of real laboratory glassware. The enhanced model achieves the highest Boundary F-score of 97.80 among compared methods, outperforming the YOLO-prompted FastSAM framework by 18.93 BF points. Furthermore, it maintains an inference speed of 7.1ms per frame and requires only 2.85% of the parameters of the closest accuracy competitor. Multi-view triangulation of mask centroids further provides 3D positions for conservative bounding-volume collision constraints. Real-robot trials achieve a 93.3% collision avoidance success rate, indicating the feasibility of the proposed perception-to-action pipeline for robot collision avoidance among fragile transparent objects. Our code is available at https://github.com/havishamy/TransYOLO_3D. Our video is available at https://havishamy.github.io/paper-videos/.

</details>


### [206] [Deliberate Before You Fly: Vision-Guided Spatial Deliberation for UAV See-and-Reach Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04825)
*Fanfu Xue,En Yu,Bohang Liu,Hongjun Wang,Yang Yang,Xindi Wang,Jiande Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DBFly是一种无人机视觉语言导航框架，通过显式的空间决策链（目标方向锚定、空间诊断、机动决策）和隐式飞行走廊引导，在连续航点生成前进行空间推理，并采用终端收敛感知的停止策略，相比SOTA基线成功率平均提升25.07个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机"看见-到达"导航方法通常直接将视觉语言表征映射为动作输出，缺乏对中间细粒度空间决策的显式建模，导致语义-控制错配，表现为机动不一致和停靠不可靠。

Method: DBFly提出三个核心组件：(1) 空间机动决策链——逐步执行目标方向锚定、空间诊断和机动决策，以高层机动意图显式引导连续航点生成；(2) 隐式飞行走廊——将初始目标方向先验转化为持久几何参考，并结合无人机当前位置在线推导走廊状态，为空间诊断和机动修正提供软几何引导；(3) 终端收敛感知停止策略——同时通过目标接近度和短时运动收敛两个维度刻画终端状态，实现更可靠的停靠。

Result: 在seen、unseen-object和unseen-scene三个测试集上，DBFly相比SOTA基线平均提升25.07个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式建模空间决策过程并引入几何引导可以有效解决视觉语言导航中的语义-控制错配问题，显著提升导航成功率和停靠可靠性。

Abstract: UAV see-and-reach navigation requires an aerial agent to approach a language-specified target visible in its initial view and stop reliably near it. Existing methods typically map vision-language representations directly to action outputs without explicitly modeling intermediate fine-grained spatial decisions. This direct mapping causes semantic-control misalignment, leading to inconsistent maneuvers and unreliable termination. To address this issue, we propose DBFly, a vision-language waypoint prediction framework that introduces explicit vision-guided spatial deliberation before waypoint generation. Specifically, DBFly introduces a spatial maneuver decision chain that progressively performs target-direction anchoring, spatial diagnosis, and maneuver decision, enabling high-level maneuver intent to explicitly guide continuous waypoint generation. DBFly further constructs an implicit flight corridor by transforming the initial target-direction prior into a persistent geometric reference and deriving an online corridor state from the UAV's current position, thereby providing soft geometric guidance for spatial diagnosis and maneuver correction. In addition, DBFly develops a terminal-convergence-aware stopping strategy that characterizes terminal states through both target proximity and short-horizon motion convergence, enabling more reliable stopping near the target. Extensive experiments across seen, unseen-object, and unseen-scene test sets demonstrate that DBFly improves the success rate over the SOTA baseline by an average of 25.07 percentage points. The project homepage is available at https://xuefanfu.github.io/DBFly-Page.

</details>


### [207] [PRIMAL3: Pathfinding via Reinforcement and Imitation Multi-Agent Learning - Leveraging LaCAM3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04905)
*Chengyang He,Tanishq Duhan,Gadiel Sznaier Camps,Fangyuan Wang,Yuhong Cao,Jiankai Sun,Ge Sun,Mac Schwager,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRIMAL3是一个超大规模、基于学习的多智能体路径规划(MAPF)框架，通过强化学习、拓扑感知通信、LaCAM3引导训练和PIBT动作精化，在关键拓扑状态（瓶颈、死端、持续冲突）下实现智能体协调，支持多达10万级智能体的城市场景，并已在物理机器人系统上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型MAPF方法在拓扑关键状态（瓶颈、死端、持续冲突）下容易失败，且难以扩展到超大规模场景，缺乏专家知识的有效引导。

Method: 1) 利用割点、死端区域、最短路距离和阻塞估计构建智能体特征；2) 构建两个互补图：同向跟随图传播多跳上下文，异向冲突图通过掩码注意力和相对特征区分竞争智能体；3) 利用策略熵识别不确定智能体，由LaCAM3提供置信度触发的动作干预和标签平滑的模仿学习目标；4) 优先级感知的PIBT模块通过持久性、学习和距离感知优先级及策略感知回退偏好精化联合动作，保证无碰撞执行；推理时无需LaCAM3。

Result: PRIMAL3大幅超越最先进的学习型基线，可扩展至10万智能体的城市级场景；物理机器人实验验证了部署可行性；消融实验验证了各组件的独立贡献。

Conclusion: 将学习探索与结构化专家指导相结合，在不依赖推理时专家求解器的情况下，实现了超大规模MAPF的显著性能提升与工程可部署性。

Abstract: We present PRIMAL3, an ultra-large-scale learning-based framework for multi-agent pathfinding (MAPF) that integrates reinforcement learning, topology-aware communication, LaCAM3-guided training, and PIBT-based action refinement. PRIMAL3 targets failures at topologically critical states, where agents must coordinate decisively around bottlenecks, dead ends, and persistent conflicts. Each agent is represented using features derived from cut vertices, dead-end regions, shortest-path distances, and blocking estimates. Two complementary graphs capture agent interactions: a same-direction following graph propagates multihop context along compatible paths, while a different-direction conflict graph differentiates agents competing for shared space through masked attention and relative features. During training, we propose to let policy entropy identify uncertain agents, for which LaCAM3 provides confidence-triggered action interventions and label-smoothed imitation targets. During execution, a priority-aware PIBT module refines the proposed joint actions using persistent, learned, and distance-aware priorities together with policy-aware fallback preferences while maintaining collision-free execution. The resulting framework combines learned exploration with structured expert guidance without requiring LaCAM3 at inference. Experiments demonstrate that PRIMAL3 substantially outperforms state-of-the-art learning-based baselines and scales to ultra-large instances with up to city-level 100,000 agents. Real-world experiments further demonstrate the feasibility of deploying PRIMAL3 on physical robotic systems and ablation studies validate the individual contributions the components we proposed. Project page: https://marmotlab.github.io/PRIMAL3/

</details>


### [208] [Mimir: A Neuro-Symbolic Memory System with Dynamic Grounding for Embodied Agents in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04933)
*Haoming Xu,Zhenlin He,Hengyi Wang,Jiafeng Xu,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Mimir是一种神经符号记忆系统，将世界记忆与任务记忆分离，并在每个动作前动态接地，以解决长程具身任务中的部分可观测性问题，在EB-ALFRED和EB-Habitat任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程具身任务要求智能体在部分可观测性下行动，同时保持场景信念和执行进度。平坦的历史记录或隐式策略状态虽包含过去观测，但缺乏显式接口来决定哪些世界事实支持当前活跃目标。

Method: Mimir将记忆分为世界记忆（对象位置、对象状态、感知证据）和任务记忆（有序目标议程、进度状态、手部状态、失败记录和执行约束）。一个接地模块在规划和执行前，将活跃目标与召回的候选世界候选绑定，填补缺失的源位置，并附加证据。

Result: 在多种骨干模型上，Mimir一致提升了EB-ALFRED和EB-Habitat任务的性能，最大提升42.5%，平均提升23.0%。与相同骨干下的最优先验方法相比，平均成功率提升8.5%。在EB-Habitat长程子集上达到86.0%成功率，显著超越闭源模型。

Conclusion: Mimir通过显式的神经符号记忆分离与动态接地机制，提供了一种有效解决长程具身任务中部分可观测性和目标追踪问题的方案，代码将开源。

Abstract: Long-horizon embodied task requires agents to act under partial observability while preserving both scene belief and execution progress. Flat histories or implicit policy states may contain past observations, but they do not provide an explicit interface for deciding which world facts support the currently active goal. We introduce Mimir, a neuro-symbolic memory that separates world memory from task memory and dynamically grounds them before each action. World memory maintains object locations, object states, and perceptual evidence, while task memory maintains an ordered goal agenda, progress state, hand state, failures, and execution constraints. A grounding module binds the active goal to recalled world candidates, fills missing source locations, and attaches evidence before planning and embodiment-specific execution. Across tested backbones, Mimir consistently improves on different EB-ALFRED and EB-Habitat tasks, with maximum gains of 42.5% and average gains of 23.0%, respectively. Compared with the best results among prior agent and memory systems evaluated under the same backbone, Mimir improves the overall average success rate by 8.5%. Finally, on the EB-Habitat Long-horizon subset, Mimir achieves 86.0% success rate, substantially outperforming current closed-source models. Our code will be released soon.

</details>


### [209] [DreamWAM: Beyond RGB Future Prediction for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04996)
*Shanglin Yuan,Weiheng Zhao,Xin Shi,Haoyi Jiang,Xianda Guo,Liu Liu,Wenyu Liu,Wei Sui,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamWAM提出一种超越RGB的结构化世界模型，通过联合预测外观、运动、几何和语义等多视角未来状态来学习动作相关表征。在LIBERO基准上，相比纯RGB基线，无滚动推理从97.30%提升至98.40%，联合推理从98.00%提升至98.90%；在LIBERO-Plus扰动下提升更显著（51.36→63.44%，69.16→75.47%），并在真实机器人操作中面对光照、背景和物体布局变化时达到74.4%成功率（基线为55.6%）。推理时禁用所有超越RGB的监督分支，仅保持RGB部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）在RGB空间中定义未来预测，但任务相关的状态转换与纹理、光照、背景、视角等无关变化纠缠在一起，导致学习到的表征对动作不敏感且鲁棒性不足。作者认为WAMs应显式预测动作相关的未来状态，而非仅依赖RGB预测。

Method: DreamWAM将未来预测重构为超越RGB的结构化世界建模：通过外观（RGB）、运动、几何和语义四个互补视角表征未来状态。训练时联合去噪RGB与运动潜在表示，并用轻量级门控残差分支处理几何与语义；VideoDiT与ActionDiT之间共享注意力机制，使动作分支从这些未来状态预测中学习。推理阶段禁用所有超越RGB的监督分支，部署仍保持RGB-only。

Result: 在LIBERO上，无滚动推理从97.30%提升至98.40%，联合视频-动作推理从98.00%提升至98.90%；在LIBERO-Plus扰动下增益更大，分别从51.36%提升至63.44%、从69.16%提升至75.47%。在真实世界的照明、背景和物体布局变化场景中，DreamWAM平均成功率达74.4%，远超Fast-WAM-Joint的55.6%。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的世界动作学习不仅取决于预测未来，更取决于以一种对动作有意义的形式来表征未来。DreamWAM通过多视角结构化未来状态预测，在不增加推理负担的前提下显著提升了动作模型的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) learn action-relevant representations by predicting how the observed world will evolve. Most existing WAMs define this future in RGB space, where task-relevant state transitions are entangled with nuisance variations in texture, illumination, background, and viewpoint. We argue that WAMs should explicitly predict action-relevant future state rather than relying on RGB prediction alone. We introduce DreamWAM, which reformulates future prediction as structured world modeling beyond RGB, representing future states through complementary views of appearance, motion, geometry, and semantics. During training, DreamWAM combines joint latent denoising of RGB and motion with lightweight gated residual branches for geometry and semantics. Shared attention between VideoDiT and ActionDiT allows the action branch to learn from these future-state predictions, while all beyond-RGB supervision branches are disabled at inference and deployment remains RGB-only. Across both no-rollout and joint video-action inference, DreamWAM consistently improves the matched RGB-only baselines on LIBERO, from 97.30\% to 98.40\% and from 98.00\% to 98.90\%, respectively. The gains become larger under unseen LIBERO-Plus perturbations, from 51.36\% to 63.44\% and from 69.16\% to 75.47\%. The same robustness extends to real-world manipulation, where DreamWAM attains an average success rate of 74.4\% across unseen changes in lighting, background, and object layout, compared with 55.6\% for Fast-WAM-Joint. These results show that robust world-action learning depends not only on predicting the future, but on representing it in a form that matters for action. The code and models are publicly released at https://github.com/hustvl/DreamWAM.

</details>


### [210] [Optimal Constrained sc-LTL Planning in MDPs via Switching Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05021)
*Zetong Xuan,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究在具有目标和安全约束（均以sc-LTL规范表达）的马尔可夫决策过程上的最优策略合成问题，提出将受约束的sc-LTL规划问题约简为扩展模型上的受约束可达性问题，并证明由单个sc-LTL规范的平稳策略构建的切换策略对最优性充分，从而可通过线性规划求解。网格世界案例验证了方法的有效性和可计算性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MDP上同时处理目标和安全约束（均以sc-LTL规范表达）的规划问题本质上是非马尔可夫的，且可能需要策略随机化来平衡目标与约束之间的权衡，这使得问题求解具有挑战性，需要一种新的最优策略合成方法。

Method: 提出一种将受约束的sc-LTL规划问题约简为扩展模型上的受约束可达性问题的框架；进而证明由各单个sc-LTL规范的平稳策略构造的一类切换策略对受约束可达性问题具有最优性充分性，从而将该问题转化为可解的线性规划。

Result: 证明了切换策略对受约束可达性问题的最优性是充分的，使得最优策略可以通过线性规划高效求解；网格世界案例研究表明，所提切换策略能够实现目标与安全约束之间的最优权衡，验证了方法的最优性与可计算性。

Conclusion: 该方法为带sc-LTL目标和安全约束的MDP规划问题提供了一种可计算的最优策略合成方案，通过约简为受约束可达性问题并利用切换策略的充分性，实现了线性规划的高效求解，兼顾了最优性和可扩展性。

Abstract: We study the synthesis of optimal policies for planning problems on Markov decision processes with both objectives and safety constraints specified in co-safe linear temporal logic (sc-LTL). Our problems are inherently non-Markovian due to the complexity of the sc-LTL specification and may require policy randomization to balance the objective and constraint. We propose a novel approach that reduces the constrained sc-LTL planning problem to a constrained reachability problem on an extended model. We then show that a class of switching policies constructed from stationary policies for the individual sc-LTL specifications is sufficient for optimality for the constrained reachability problem. Our finding enables a tractable linear program to compute the optimal policy. A grid world case study demonstrates that our switching policies can achieve the optimal trade-off between the objective and the safety constraint and validates both optimality and tractability.

</details>


### [211] [BridgeVLA++: A Data-Efficient, Generalizable, and Memory-Augmented Vision-Language-Action Framework for 3D Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05042)
*Peiyan Li,Yuze Zhu,Yixiang Chen,Qisen Ma,Yuan Xu,Jiabing Yang,He Guan,Yan Huang,Hongtao Wu,Xiao Ma,Tao Kong,Liang Wang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BridgeVLA++是一个统一的3D视觉-语言-动作框架，通过引入统一的时空记忆架构来扩展之前的BridgeVLA模型，使其同时具备数据高效学习、稳健泛化和记忆感知的机器人操作能力，在空间操作任务和记忆依赖操作基准上均取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D VLA方法存在三个主要局限：数据需求量大、在分布偏移下泛化能力有限、缺乏对过去观测的显式记忆。这些局限阻碍了其在数据稀缺、开放世界和记忆依赖的机器人操作场景中的应用。

Method: BridgeVLA++在BridgeVLA的基础上，引入统一的时空记忆架构，建模持久空间上下文和时间交互历史。BridgeVLA通过将原始点云投影为多视角图像并预测中间热图再生成动作，保持预训练VLM的输入输出对齐，从而提高数据效率和泛化能力。记忆增强框架在保持这些优势的同时，使模型能够对观测历史进行推理。

Result: 实验表明该框架在空间操作任务上表现优异且具有稳健泛化能力，在两个具有挑战性的记忆依赖操作基准上达到最先进性能，同时不牺牲BridgeVLA原有的数据效率和泛化能力。此外在双臂操作设置和额外的真实机器人平台上均验证了有效性。

Conclusion: BridgeVLA++被确立为一个统一的3D视觉-语言-动作框架，能够同时支持数据高效学习、稳健泛化和有效的记忆感知机器人操作，且在不同任务、环境和机器人平台上具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Leveraging pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) to construct vision-language-action (VLA) models has emerged as a promising paradigm for 3D robot manipulation. However, existing 3D VLA methods remain data-hungry, exhibit limited generalization under distribution shifts, and lack explicit memory of past observations. These limitations hinder their application to data-scarce, open-world, and memory-dependent manipulation scenarios. Our previous work, BridgeVLA, improves data efficiency and generalization by preserving the input--output alignment of a pre-trained VLM during 3D action learning: raw point clouds are projected into multi-view images, and intermediate heatmaps are predicted before generating robot actions. In this work, we develop BridgeVLA++ by equipping BridgeVLA with a unified spatio-temporal memory architecture that models persistent spatial context and temporal interaction history. The resulting memory-augmented framework can reason over observation histories while preserving BridgeVLA's data efficiency and generalization capabilities. Extensive experiments show that our framework achieves strong performance on spatial manipulation tasks while exhibiting robust generalization. BridgeVLA++ further achieves state-of-the-art performance on two challenging memory-dependent manipulation benchmarks without sacrificing the data efficiency and generalization of the original BridgeVLA. In addition, BridgeVLA++ performs effectively in bimanual manipulation settings and is validated on an additional real-world robotic platform, demonstrating its scalability across tasks, environments, and robotic platforms. These results establish BridgeVLA++ as a unified 3D vision-language-action framework that simultaneously supports data-efficient learning, robust generalization, and effective memory-aware robot manipulation. Project website: https://bridgevla-plus.github.io/.

</details>


### [212] [SpikingNav: Robust Embodied Navigation with Spiking Neural Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05078)
*Jiahong Zhang,Sijun Shen,Dehua Wu,Yifan Lin,Xuechen Xia,Xu Chu,Youhui Zhang,GuoqiLi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SpikingNav，一个基于脉冲神经网络（SNN）的鲁棒室内具身导航框架，包含脉冲感知编码器（SSE）和脉冲策略网络（SPN），在PointNav和ObjectNav任务上实现了与ANN基线相当的干净性能，同时展现出更强的鲁棒性，参数更少、每步计算量更低，并在Thruster-V2神经形态芯片上完成了硬件验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于ANN的导航模型虽然性能强劲，但依赖密集计算且在视觉损坏下性能退化严重。SNN具有事件驱动计算和内在时间动态特性，有望在资源受限平台上实现紧凑且鲁棒的导航，但脉冲感知和策略动态是否能提升视觉丰富环境下具身导航的鲁棒性仍是开放问题，因此需要系统性地探索SNN在具身导航中的潜力。

Method: SpikingNav包含两个核心组件：1）Spiking Sensing Encoder（SSE），采用脉冲骨干网络提取任务条件化的视觉特征；2）Spiking Policy Network（SPN），通过膜积分、阈值触发和脉冲重置来维护循环策略状态。整体框架利用SNN的动态特性和脉冲激活机制来提升导航性能和鲁棒性，并在PointNav和ObjectNav任务上于干净观测和视觉损坏场景下进行评估。

Result: 在干净观测下，SpikingNav与匹配的ANN基线性能相当，但参数更少、每步计算量更低。在视觉损坏下，SpikingNav将ObjectNav成功率从31.05%提升至34.12%，平均成功率从8.45%提升至13.71%。此外，SpikingNav的脉冲感知方法成功部署在Thruster-V2神经形态芯片上，通过了物理硬件验证。

Conclusion: SpikingNav证明了基于脉冲的感知与策略动态能够有效提升具身导航的鲁棒性，同时降低计算开销，并且可以在真实神经形态硬件上实例化，为资源受限和信息物理系统中的鲁棒具身导航提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Embodied navigation requires an agent to make sequential decisions from egocentric observations in a physical environment. Existing Artificial Neural Network (ANN)-based navigation models have achieved strong performance, yet they often rely on dense computation and may degrade under visual corruptions. Spiking neural networks (SNNs) provide event-driven computation and intrinsic temporal dynamics, which are promising for compact and robust navigation on resource-constrained platforms. However, whether spike-based sensing and policy dynamics can improve robustness in visually rich embodied navigation remains an open problem. This paper proposes SpikingNav, a spiking framework for robust indoor embodied navigation. It contains a Spiking Sensing Encoder (SSE) and a Spiking Policy Network (SPN). The SSE extracts task-conditioned visual features with a spike-based backbone. The SPN maintains a recurrent policy state through membrane integration, thresholding, and spike-triggered reset. In this way, SpikingNav exploits the dynamic properties and spike activations of SNNs to improve navigation performance and robustness. We evaluate SpikingNav on PointNav and ObjectNav under clean observations and visual corruptions. SpikingNav achieves competitive clean performance and stronger robustness with fewer parameters and lower per-step computation than a matched ANN baseline. For instance, SpikingNav improves ObjectNav success from 31.05% to 34.12%, and raises the average success under visual corruptions from 8.45% to 13.71%, demonstrating the benefits of spike-based sensing and policy dynamics. We further validate the deployability of our spike-based sensing method on the Thruster-V2 neuromorphic chip. This physical hardware validation shows that SpikingNav can be instantiated on a real neuromorphic substrate for cyber-physical systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [C$^2$MOE: Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts for Incomplete Multimodal Emotion Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04013)
*Yuntao Shou,Tao Meng,Wei Ai,Keqin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出C$^2$MOE框架，一个基于一致性与互补性引导的混合专家系统，用于不完整多模态情感识别。该框架通过信息论方法将多模态知识分解为一致性和互补性成分，并采用双分支预测机制和可学习的动态重加权模块实现鲁棒的不完整模态推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感识别在真实场景中常面临模态缺失问题，导致模型性能严重退化。现有方法仅通过跨模态一致性学习增强鲁棒性，忽略了模态互补性，从而产生有偏的重构结果。

Method: C$^2$MOE框架包括：(1) 通过交互感知专家将多模态知识分解为一致性与互补性成分，一致性通过最大化跨模态可预测性捕获，互补性通过最大化模态间条件熵保留；(2) 双分支预测机制：一致性分支通过最小化不确定性对齐缺失特征与联合分布，互补性分支通过熵最大化利用模态特有线索；(3) 可学习的动态重加权模块，自适应分配各专家输出的重要性分数以进行鲁棒融合。

Result: 在多个多模态情感识别基准数据集上，C$^2$MOE在各种不同模态缺失设置下均一致性地超越了现有的最先进方法，验证了其鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: C$^2$MOE通过统一表示学习与缺失模态填补于一个信息论框架中，证明同时建模模态一致性与互补性对于不完整多模态情感识别至关重要，为该领域提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) highlight its reliance on complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world data often suffer from missing modalities due to transmission errors or user behavior, severely degrading model performance. Existing methods enhance robustness via cross-modal consistency learning but largely ignore modality complementarity, leading to biased reconstructions. To address this limitation, we propose C$2$MOE, a novel Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts framework for incomplete multimodal emotion learning. Our approach unifies representation learning and missing modality imputation within a principled information-theoretic framework. Specifically, multimodal knowledge is factorized into consistency and complementarity components via interaction-aware experts. Consistency is captured by maximizing cross-modal predictability, while complementarity is preserved by maximizing conditional entropy between modalities. Building upon this decomposition, C$2$MOE introduces a dual-branch prediction mechanism for robust imputation under missing modalities. The consistency branch aligns imputed features with the joint distribution by minimizing uncertainty, and the complementarity branch exploits modality-unique cues via entropy maximization. Finally, C$2$MOE employs a learnable reweighting module that dynamically assigns importance scores to each expert's output, yielding a robust and adaptive fusion for imputation. Extensive experiments on multiple MERC benchmarks demonstrate that C$2$MOE consistently surpasses state-of-the-art methods across various missing-modality settings, validating its robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [214] [On Hamming-Lipschitz Type Stability of the Subdominant (Minmax) Ultrametric: Theory and Simple Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04014)
*Alokendu Mazumder,Arnab Roy,Punit Rathore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对次主导（minmax）超度量算子的稳定性问题，提出了一种ℓ₀型（稀疏扰动）稳定性理论。研究揭示稀疏编辑的传播仅通过最小生成树（MST）进行：成对超度量值仅在树路径穿越被编辑边或暴露切割时才会改变。文章给出了尖锐的每次编辑暴露切割得分和仅依赖树的全局包络，推得Hamming-Lipschitz界，并证明了这些结果在严格切割分离下的最优性，以及一个条件近可加性原则。实验验证了结构得分在深层嵌入图的层次表示脆弱性诊断中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的次主导超度量稳定性理论通常以ℓ∞或Gromov–Hausdorff度量来表述，但这些界对仅改变少数成对距离的稀疏扰动并不适用，无法精确刻画稀疏编辑如何影响超度量结构。因此需要发展一套专门针对稀疏扰动的稳定性理论。

Method: 作者开发了ℓ₀型稳定性理论。核心思想是通过最小生成树（MST）来追踪编辑的传播路径：定义一个成对的超度量值仅在树路径穿越被编辑边或被编辑的非树边新暴露的切割时才会改变。基于此推导出每次编辑的暴露切割得分、树仅相关的全局包络以及Hamming-Lipschitz上界。此外还证明了尖锐性结果（严格切割分离下树边界恰好可达；非树边编辑存在单次编辑改变Θ(n²)个超度量条目的显式族），并提出多编辑下的条件近可加性原则。

Result: 在理论上，得到了稀疏扰动下超度量变化的精确传播机制（仅通过MST传播）、锐利的每个编辑暴露切割得分、树仅全局包络、Hamming-Lipschitz界以及匹配的锐利性下界；在条件编辑场景下证明了近可加性原则。实验上，在深层嵌入图上验证了结构得分可作为层次表示的脆弱性诊断工具。

Conclusion: ℓ₀型稳定性理论为稀疏扰动下超度量的变化提供了精确且锐利的刻画，证明了这种与树几何的依赖关系是不可避免的，并展示了该理论在层次表示脆弱性分析中的实用价值。

Abstract: The subdominant (minmax) ultrametric is a canonical tree-structured summary of a dissimilarity matrix, arising equivalently as the ultrametric induced by single-linkage clustering. While its classical stability theory is usually formulated in $\ell_\infty$ or Gromov--Hausdorff terms, such bounds are poorly suited to sparse perturbations that alter only a few pairwise distances. We develop an $\ell_0$-type stability theory for this operator. Our analysis shows that sparse edits propagate only through the minimum spanning tree (MST): a pairwise ultrametric value can change only if its tree path crosses an edited edge or a cut newly exposed by an edited off-tree edge. This yields a sharp per-edit exposed-cut score and a tree-only global envelope, leading to Hamming--Lipschitz bounds on the number of ultrametric entries that can change. We also prove sharpness results showing that this dependence on tree geometry is unavoidable: under strict cut separation the tree-edge bound is attained exactly, and for off-tree edits there are explicit families in which one edited distance changes $Θ(n^2)$ ultrametric entries. In addition, we prove a conditional near-additivity principle for multiple edits under certified large per-edit changed regions and negligible aggregate overlap. Experiments on deep-embedding graphs show that the resulting structural scores provide useful vulnerability diagnostics for hierarchical representations.

</details>


### [215] [A Trust-region Framework for Moment Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04026)
*Oluwasegun A. Somefun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个信任域框架（trust-region framework）来统一理解Adam等自适应矩估计优化器，由此推导出一族基于二阶矩估计和归一化p阶矩估计的学习率机制（Gmake）。当p=4时涉及峰度类估计。实验表明，在信任域约束较弱时四阶矩实现优势最大，随着约束增强，二阶矩实现逐渐更具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam等自适应矩估计机制在随机梯度优化中被广泛使用，但缺乏一个统一的框架来解释其行为，尤其是不同阶矩估计在不同条件下各自优劣势的机理尚不清晰。

Method: 构建信任域框架，将每个权重的更新步长约束在由p阶（p∈[2,4]）矩约束控制的信任域内，由此推导出一族学习率机制Gmake，涵盖二阶矩估计和归一化p阶矩估计。当p=4时涉及峰度类（kurtosis-like）估计。该框架统一解释了矩估计归一化、学习率调度、动量作为谱低通滤波以及算子级谱归一化。

Result: 在GPT2-124M（训练于FineWeb-Edu和TinyStories）上的实验表明：信任域约束较弱时，四阶矩（峰度类）实现的优势最大；当信任域约束逐步增强时，二阶矩实现变得更具竞争力，通常获得略低于四阶矩实现的验证损失。

Conclusion: 信任域框架为自适应优化器提供了统一的理论理解，p=2和p=4两端的矩估计实现之间存在权衡：弱约束选择四阶矩，强约束选择二阶矩，为该类优化器的设计提供了指导性洞见。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a trust-region framework for understanding the behavior of adaptive moment estimation mechanisms, such as \textsc{Adam}, in stochastic gradient optimization. Specifically, in this framework, the magnitude of the update step for each individual weight is constrained within a trust-region governed by a moment constraint of order $p\in[2,4]$. The resulting derivation then leads to a family of learning-rate mechanisms based on second-moment estimation and a normalized $p$-th moment estimation. When $p=4$, this involves kurtosis-like estimation. The general mechanism, referred to as \textsc{Gmake}, provides a unified interpretation of normalization by moment estimation, learning-rate scheduling, spectral lowpass filtering as momentum, and operator-level spectral normalization within a common trust-region framework. Experiments on GPT2-124M trained on FineWeb-Edu and TinyStories suggest that the fourth-moment realization provides its greatest benefit when trust-region constraints are weak. As progressively stronger trust-region controls are introduced, the second-moment realization becomes increasingly competitive, often achieving slightly lower validation loss than its corresponding fourth-moment realization.

</details>


### [216] [Learning to Resolve Neutron Resonances with Fully Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04027)
*Nataly R. Panczyk,Athanasios Stamatopoulos,Josef Svoboda,Majdi I. Radaideh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨将全卷积神经网络与R-Matrix代码结合，用于自动检测中子透射谱中的共振峰。尽管在七个透射谱（两个评估、五个实验）上分类准确率达93%，但进一步分析表明该指标高估了模型的泛化能力，模型无法可靠地推广到未见过的同位素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统R-Matrix代码拟合中子透射数据依赖先验评估和大量人工操作，且传统峰值识别方法难以处理复杂、带噪的中子透射数据，需要自动化方法来加速实验后处理并减少对先验评估的依赖所带来的偏差。

Method: 采用全卷积神经网络（FCN）对透射谱中的每个点进行逐点分类，判断其属于共振区还是非共振区。使用七个透射谱（两个评估数据、五个实验数据）进行训练和测试，并在前期PHYSOR 2026分析基础上纳入更多训练数据。

Result: 模型在训练数据上实现了约93%的分类准确率，但进一步分析表明准确率指标高估了其泛化能力。尽管增加了训练数据，模型仍不能可靠地推广到此前未见过的同位素。

Conclusion: 单纯增加训练数据不足以实现可靠的跨同位素泛化。未来工作应探索更大、更多样化的训练数据集，并将中子共振的已知物理特征融入模型以提升性能。

Abstract: This work investigates the feasibility of augmenting traditional R-Matrix codes with a robust machine learning framework for automatically detecting neutron resonances in transmission spectra. Neutron transmission data are often complex and noisy, making them difficult to analyze using traditional peak-identification methods. The state-of-the-art R-Matrix codes currently used by physicists to fit these data often depend on prior evaluations and require substantial manual effort. This preliminary study demonstrates a method for accelerating the post-experimental processing of neutron transmission data and reducing bias associated with dependence on prior evaluations. We employ a fully convolutional neural network to classify individual points as belonging to resonance or non-resonance regions in seven transmission spectra---two evaluated and five experimental. Although the model achieves classification accuracies in the range of 93\%, further analysis shows that this metric overstates its ability to generalize. Building on our prior analysis in PHYSOR 2026, we find that, despite the inclusion of additional training data, the method does not generalize reliably to previously unseen isotopes. To address these limitations, future work should evaluate whether a larger and more diverse training dataset can produce a generalizable model and should incorporate known physical characteristics of neutron resonances to improve model performance.

</details>


### [217] [Lindblad-Inspired Multi-Timescale Reservoir Computing with Separable Rotation and Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04028)
*Jyotiranjan Beuria,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Lindblad开放系统原理的多时间尺度回声状态网络（储层计算）架构，通过精确离散化的阻尼旋转模式构建循环算子，使旋转（相位混合）与衰减（记忆遗忘）成为独立可调的设计变量，同时通过衰减谱直接保证回声状态稳定性，在多个基准测试中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回声状态网络（ESN）将信号混合、记忆保持和稳定性约束耦合在单个随机循环矩阵中，无法分别调控可逆混合与不可逆遗忘，且通常依赖事后谱半径缩放来保证稳定性。现有结构化设计虽改善了拓扑、范数保持、泄漏或深度，但缺乏对可逆混合与不可逆遗忘的独立模态控制，同时难以提供直接的全局稳定性保证。

Method: 受经典Lindblad开放量子系统动力学启发，构建多时间尺度储层。循环算子由精确离散化的阻尼旋转模态组装而成，旋转与衰减成为独立的控制变量，分别主导相位混合和记忆丧失。正交模态混合保持矩阵的规范性（normality），衰减谱直接决定回声状态稳定性裕度，无需事后谱半径重缩放。

Result: 在十个对齐随机种子下，与标准、泄漏、深度、正交、循环、下一代储层以及紧凑训练的GRU进行对比，覆盖线性记忆、非线性递归、混沌预测、延迟逻辑和真实传感器校准等任务。该储层在有界NARMA-20上取得最佳固定储层性能，在Lorenz-63上取得最低平均误差，在线性记忆任务上与最强结果持平，并在广泛基准中保持竞争力。消融研究显示旋转增加状态多样性，耗散提供受控遗忘并改善预测条件性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可解释的循环架构，其中混合、记忆和稳定性是显式且独立可调的设计变量，弥合了开放系统动力学原理与结构化状态空间建模之间的鸿沟，为储层计算提供了新的理论支撑和实用方法。

Abstract: Echo-state networks enable efficient temporal learning by fixing the recurrent dynamics and training only a linear readout. However, conventional reservoirs typically accommodate signal mixing, memory retention, and stability within a single random recurrent matrix. Existing structured designs improve topology, norm preservation, leakage, or depth, but generally do not provide separate modal control of reversible mixing and irreversible forgetting together with a direct global stability guarantee. We introduce a classical Lindblad-inspired multi-timescale reservoir that bridges open-system dynamical principles with structured state-space modeling. The recurrent operator is assembled from exactly discretized damped rotational modes, so rotation and decay become independent design variables governing phase mixing and memory loss. Orthogonal mode mixing preserves normality, while the decay spectrum directly determines the echo-state stability margin without post-hoc spectral-radius rescaling. We evaluate the method over ten aligned seeds against standard, leaky, deep, orthogonal, cycle, and next-generation reservoirs, together with a compact trained gated recurrent unit, across linear memory, nonlinear recurrence, chaotic forecasting, delayed logic, and real sensor calibration. Across the benchmark suite, the proposed reservoir achieves the best fixed-reservoir performance on bounded NARMA-20 and the lowest mean error on Lorenz-63, matches the strongest linear-memory result, and remains broadly competitive across broad range of benchmarks. Ablation studies show that rotation increases state diversity, whereas dissipation provides controlled forgetting and improves predictive conditioning. The resulting framework offers an interpretable recurrent architecture in which mixing, memory, and stability are explicit and independently tunable design variables.

</details>


### [218] [An Explainable LLM Agent Layer for Open-World Anomaly Detection in Oil Wells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04041)
*Lucas Gouveia Omena Lopes,Thales Miranda de Almeida Vieira,Eduardo Toledo de Lima Junior,William Wagner Matos Lira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在现有开放世界学习（OWL）油井异常检测流水线下游引入了一个大语言模型（LLM）智能体层（基于Qwen3.5-397B-A17B MoE模型，经NVIDIA NIM部署），用于弥补上游方法缺乏解释性和人类可读新颖性命名的不足。在3W数据集的989个真实井段上，该智能体在九类分类中达到35.1% top-1 / 63.9% top-3精度，新颖性检测达89.7%且5/7隐藏类可稳定命名。该智能体作为上游方法的"伴侣"而非替代品，提供证据支撑的决策确认、可审计的自然语言解释、矛盾标记和新颖性命名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OWL流水线（自动编码器检测+多类分类+Mahalanobis新颖性检测）能回答"发生了什么"，但无法解释"模型为何如此判断"或"操作员接下来该做什么"，也不能为发现的新颖性簇赋予人类可读的名称。这种可解释性缺口阻碍了OWL流水线在油田运营场景中的实际部署。

Method: 在已发布的OWL流水线下游放置LLM智能体层，采用Qwen3.5-397B-A17B Mixture-of-Experts模型经NVIDIA NIM服务部署。智能体接收结构化传感器指标及上游分类/新颖性断言，输出自然语言正当化理由、置信度排序的批评意见，以及对检测到的新颖性进行合并命名。智能体的角色包括：(1)传感器证据支持时确认上游决策；(2)以传感器为基础的语言证成决策供操作员审计；(3)上游标签不合理时标记分歧；(4)为新颖性簇命名，使未标注事件以人类可读标签呈现给工程师。

Result: 基于3W数据集的989个真实井段进行三项研究：九类全分类top-1精度35.1%、top-3精度63.9%（95%CI [56.9, 70.4]）；7个探测类top-2验证精度71.7%（[64.8, 77.6]），精确率0.91（[0.84, 0.95]）；新颖性检测89.7%（[87.0, 91.9]），7个隐藏类中有5个可稳定命名。结果表明智能体定位为确认与解释工具而非独立分类器。

Conclusion: LLM智能体层能有效弥合OWL流水线的可解释性缺口，在不替代上游方法的前提下提供可审计的决策解释和人类可读的新颖性命名，为OWL流水线在油田运营环境中的部署扫清了关键障碍。

Abstract: Open-World Learning (OWL) pipelines for oil well anomaly detection have recently been shown to combine autoencoder-based detection, multiclass classification, and Mahalanobis-based novelty detection on the public 3W dataset. These pipelines answer \textit{what happened}, but they do not explain \textit{why the model believes it} or \textit{what the operator should do next}, and they do not put a human-readable name on the novelty clusters they discover. This paper evaluates a Large Language Model (LLM) agent layer placed downstream of the OWL pipeline, designed as a \textbf{companion} to the published upstream methods rather than a replacement. Using the Qwen3.5-397B-A17B Mixture-of-Experts model served via NVIDIA NIM, the agent receives structured sensor metrics and upstream classification or novelty assertions, and returns natural-language justifications, confidence-ranked critiques, and consolidated names for detected novelties. Across three studies spanning 989 real well-file segments from the 3W dataset, the agent achieved $35.1\%$ top-1 / $63.9\%$ top-3 (95\% CI [56.9, 70.4]) classification on all nine classes, $71.7\%$ top-2 validation [64.8, 77.6] with precision $0.91$ [0.84, 0.95] across 7 probed classes, and $89.7\%$ novelty detection [87.0, 91.9] with stable cluster naming on 5 of 7 hidden classes. The agent is not a standalone classifier. Its role is to: (1) confirm upstream decisions when sensor evidence supports them, (2) justify decisions in sensor-grounded language operators can audit, (3) flag disagreement when upstream labels are implausible, and (4) name novelties so that clustered unlabeled events arrive at the engineer with a consolidated human-readable label. The goal is to close the explainability gap that currently blocks deployment of OWL pipelines in operational settings.

</details>


### [219] [Tactus: Open-Vocabulary Object Recognition from Low-Cost Pressure Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04043)
*Abdul Basit Tonmoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tactus是一个基于电阻式压力阵列的开放触觉表示学习模型，仅凭压力数据即可回答文本查询，在STAG基准上达到0.771±0.062的top-1准确率（top-3为0.935），匹配并有时超越监督式闭集CNN（0.76），且无需训练分类头。该模型使用仅187条训练录音、在144k无标签同传感器帧上进行掩码自编码器预训练，并利用传感器自身的标定仿射变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电阻式压力阵列是最便宜且出货量最大的触觉传感器，但现有触觉表示学习研究几乎全部集中在光学传感器（对变形凝胶成像）上。作者旨在填补这一空白，为电阻式压力阵列建立开放、有效的触觉表示学习模型。

Method: Tactus采用一个小数据配方：1）仅用187条训练录音；2）在144k无标签同传感器帧上进行掩码自编码器（MAE）预训练；3）利用传感器自身的标定仿射变换（该变换恢复的准确率超过所有架构改动之和）。模型直接从压力数据回答文本查询，无需训练分类头。

Result: 在STAG基准（27个物体，held-out录音）上，Tactus达到0.771±0.062 top-1（四次运行），top-3为0.935，匹配并有时超越数据集的有监督闭集CNN（0.76）。误差集中在少数接触模糊类别，与文本目标几何无关（Spearman rho≤0.05），对改写甚至裸名称查询稳健。同时诚实报告失败：跨传感器预训练池化无增益、视觉协同训练损害触觉、错误归一化的输入管道静默丢弃了97%的动态范围。

Conclusion: Tactus证明电阻式压力阵列可以通过小数据和轻量配方实现与监督CNN相当的文本查询感知能力，模型权重、代码和记忆层均开放发布。研究同时强调了对失败案例透明报告的重要性，为触觉表示学习在该类低成本传感器上的可行性提供了实证支撑。

Abstract: Resistive pressure arrays are the cheapest and most widely shipped tactile sensors, yet tactile representation learning has concentrated on optical sensors that image a deforming gel. We present Tactus, an open model that answers text queries from pressure data alone: on the STAG benchmark (27 objects, held-out recordings), it reaches 0.771 +/- 0.062 top-1 over four runs (top-3 0.935), matching, and at best exceeding, the dataset's supervised closed-set CNN at 0.76, with no trained classifier head. The recipe is small-data: 187 training recordings, masked-autoencoder pretraining on 144k unlabeled same-sensor frames, and the sensor's own calibration affine, which recovered more accuracy than every architecture change combined. The released model's errors concentrate in a few contact-ambiguous classes, are uncorrelated with text-target geometry (Spearman rho <= 0.05 over 702 class pairs), and survive paraphrased and even bare-name queries within one point; two diverse frames recover 89% of eight-frame accuracy. Failures are reported with equal precision: cross-sensor pretraining pooling gave no gain, vision co-training degraded touch, and a mis-normalized input pipeline silently discarded 97% of the sensor's dynamic range while producing plausible intermediate results. Weights, code, and the memory layer the model plugs into are released openly.

</details>


### [220] [Robust and Personalized Federated Learning for Aircraft-Engine Prognostics under Benign and Adversarial Client Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04045)
*Chinmoy Mitra,Md. Mehedi Hasan Nipu,Mohammad Sakib Mahmood,Md. Rakibul Islam,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习中两类异构性挑战（良性异构与对抗性异构）对航空发动机剩余使用寿命（RUL）预测模型的影响，通过多任务一维CNN和C-MAPSS数据集的非IID划分，评估了四种个性化方法、五种攻击和四种聚合方法，发现共享表征个性化可缩小约70%的本地-集中式误差差距，而Krum聚合对协调攻击最具鲁棒性，结合个性化与鲁棒聚合可在保持精度的同时将攻击成功率降至2.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习允许飞机机队运营商在不共享原始数据的情况下联合训练RUL模型，但面临两类挑战：良性异构（不同运营商工况和故障模式不同）和对抗性异构（被攻陷的运营商提交有毒更新）。现有工作缺乏对这两类挑战的系统性安全导向评估，尤其是精度指标无法真正反映模型安全性，需要显式评估攻击成功率。

Method: 采用多任务一维卷积神经网络（multi-task 1D-CNN），在C-MAPSS基准上构造结构化的非IID数据划分。对比四种处理良性异构的方法（共享表征个性化、近端正则化、服务端重新加权等），并评估五种攻击（含物理动机的传感器值后门攻击）对四种聚合方法（标准平均、Krum等）的影响。

Result: 共享表征个性化缩小了约70%的本地-集中化RMSE差距（远优于近端正则化的21%和服务端重新加权的10%）；传感器值后门攻击对标准平均聚合的成功率达94.9%且不影响干净精度；Krum将攻击成功率降低一个数量级，是唯一能抵御协调攻击者的聚合器；结合个性化与鲁棒聚合可将攻击成功率降至2.8%，仅带来轻微精度损失。结果在客户端数量和更难的六工况数据集上保持一致。

Conclusion: 精度指标不能单独作为模型安全性的认证标准，必须显式评估攻击成功率；个性化本身不提供恶意更新防护，须与鲁棒聚合结合才能在保持性能的同时实现安全；这揭示了鲁棒更新选择与协作表征学习之间的权衡。代码与数据划分已开源以支持可复现性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables aircraft fleet operators to jointly train remaining-useful-life (RUL) models from engine sensor telemetry without sharing raw data. This study examines two complementary challenges: benign heterogeneity, where honest operators observe different operating conditions and fault modes, and adversarial heterogeneity, where compromised operators submit poisoned updates. We conduct a controlled, safety-oriented evaluation using a multi-task one-dimensional convolutional neural network and a structurally non-IID partition of the Commercial Modular Aero-Propulsion System Simulation (C-MAPSS) benchmark. We compare four remedies for benign heterogeneity and evaluate five attacks against four aggregation methods, including a physically motivated sensor-value backdoor designed to mask engine degradation.
  Shared-representation personalization closes approximately 70% of the local-to-centralized root-mean-square-error gap, compared with 21% for proximal regularization and 10% for server-side reweighting. The backdoor achieves a 94.9% attack success rate against standard averaging while leaving clean accuracy statistically unchanged, demonstrating that accuracy alone cannot certify model safety and that attack success must be evaluated explicitly. Krum reduces attack success by an order of magnitude and is the only evaluated aggregator that withstands coordinated attackers, whereas personalization alone provides no protection. Combining personalization with robust aggregation restores robustness (2.8% attack success) with only a small accuracy cost, revealing a trade-off between robust update selection and collaborative representation learning. Results remain consistent across client counts and on a harder six-condition dataset. Code and data partitions are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [221] [Recurrent Residual Quantization: A Progressive Multi-Precision Representation for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04048)
*Yu Luo,Bo Dong,Wenhua Cheng,Haihao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了递归残差量化（RRQ），一种后训练量化框架，将权重表示为低比特量化基加一系列量化残差修正，从而从单一检查点实现多种有效精度（2/4/6/8比特），且无需校准数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同部署场景对LLM的精度、内存占用和吞吐量有着多样化的约束需求，而传统量化方法通常需要为每个目标位宽单独保存一个检查点，造成存储和部署开销过大。

Method: RRQ首先通过PTQ或舍入到最近（RTN）得到2比特的量化基模型，然后逐步添加由RTN生成的低开销2比特残差，以此构建4、6、8比特表示。该方法无需校准数据，也不需要联合多比特优化。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，完整的全RTN 2/4/6/8比特包构建耗时1,293秒，比MatGPTQ快3.3倍。在六个近期LLM上的实验显示在6和8比特下具有竞争力的精度，4比特下表现因模型而异。

Conclusion: RRQ提供了一种灵活高效的量化方案，使得单一检查点即可支持多种精度部署，在构建速度和精度之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Serving large language models (LLMs) under diverse deployment constraints requires flexible trade-offs between accuracy, memory footprint, and throughput. However, conventional quantization methods typically require a separate checkpoint for each target bit-width. We introduce Recurrent Residual Quantization (RRQ), a post-training quantization (PTQ) framework that represents weights as a low-bit quantized base together with a sequence of quantized residual corrections, enabling multiple effective precisions from a single checkpoint. Starting from a 2-bit model obtained via post-training quantization (PTQ) or round-to-nearest (RTN), RRQ progressively adds lightweight 2-bit residuals generated via RTN to construct 4-, 6-, and 8-bit representations. The method is calibration-free and avoids joint multi-bit optimization. In our Qwen3-8B setup, the full all-RTN 2-/4-/6-/8-bit package is constructed in 1,293 seconds, 3.3 times faster than the measured MatGPTQ construction. Experiments on six recent LLMs show competitive accuracy at 6 and 8 bits, with model-dependent behavior at 4 bits. The code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [222] [CAMP: A Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04051)
*Jung Min Choi,Vijaya Krishna yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAMP是一个面向长期时序预测的周期感知多尺度补丁混合器框架，通过自适应周期学习、边界引导补丁混合和多分辨率残差建模，在七个基准上取得最优或并列最优的MSE/MAE结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有周期感知预测器通常在数据集级别选择单一周期，无法适应随时间变化的周期行为或多周期共存的情况；基于补丁的模型对所有补丁位置统一处理，忽略了远离预测边界的补丁需要更广的上下文精化而近期补丁应更直接保留信息的需求；去周期后的残差动力学跨越多个时间分辨率，单一尺度难以充分刻画。

Method: CAMP包含三个核心模块：(1) 自适应周期学习模块，为每个输入窗口分别识别主导频率并生成历史和未来的周期分量，无需预定义周期长度；(2) 边界引导补丁混合器，引入位置依赖的精化机制，使早期补丁整合更广的时间上下文，同时保留靠近预测边界的信息；(3) 通过时间对齐的多分辨率表示对去周期残差建模，在单一框架内捕获不同尺度的互补动态。

Result: 在七个长期预测基准上，CAMP在六个数据集上取得最佳平均MSE，在六个数据集上取得最佳或并列最佳MAE；在四个PEMS交通基准的十六个设置中取得最高MSE胜场数。

Conclusion: CAMP通过自适应周期发现、位置感知的补丁混合以及多尺度残差建模三个互补机制，有效解决了真实世界时序中周期动态变化、多周期共存以及多分辨率残差模式等挑战，展示了周期感知多尺度建模在长期预测中的显著优势。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often governed by recurring patterns, but their dominant periods may vary across datasets, forecasting settings, and individual input windows. Existing cycle-aware forecasters commonly rely on a single period selected at the dataset level, which can be restrictive when periodic behavior changes over time or when multiple cycles coexist. Moreover, patch-based models typically process all patch positions uni- formly, although patches farther from the forecast boundary may require broader contextual refinement, while recent patches contain information that should be preserved more directly. Af- ter cyclic behavior is removed, the remaining dynamics may also span multiple temporal resolutions and cannot be adequately de- scribed at a single scale. We introduce CAMP, a Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer designed to address these challenges. The Adaptive Cycle Learning module identifies dominant fre- quencies separately for each input window and generates both historical and future cyclic components without requiring a pre- defined cycle length. The Horizon-Guided Patch Mixer intro- duces position-dependent refinement, allowing earlier patches to incorporate broader temporal context while preserving infor- mation close to the forecast boundary. CAMP further models the de-cycled residual through temporally aligned multi-resolution representations, enabling complementary dynamics at different scales to be captured within one forecasting framework. Across seven long-term forecasting benchmarks, CAMP achieves the best average MSE on six datasets and the best or tied-best MAE on six. It also obtains the highest MSE win count across sixteen settings on four PEMS traffic benchmarks.

</details>


### [223] [LaPrune: Controllable Differentiable Sparsity at Million Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04057)
*Jakub Antczak,Joanna Wojciechowicz,Łukasz Struski,Jacek Tabor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LaPrune，一种具有精确预算约束的可微分稀疏层，通过LapSum屏障保持选择质量，并利用归一化二阶矩约束将掩码从密集等质量分配推向硬top-k选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬top-k选择会阻断梯度传播，而现有的连续松弛方法往往将掩码的"硬度"与所选质量（即top-k预算）耦合在一起，难以在保持精确预算的同时实现可微训练。

Method: 引入LaPrune层：使用LapSum屏障函数保证掩码的选择质量（即top-k预算）不受损失，同时施加归一化二阶矩约束，使掩码从均匀的密集分配逐渐演化为硬top-k选择。推导了饱和分数的总体预测、近似二值极限律，以及近零分数的紧致最坏情况保证。

Result: 证明了归一化硬度参数对分数尺度具有不变性，而固定的LapSum温度参数则不具备这种不变性。提出了对掩码饱和分数和近零分数的理论保证。

Conclusion: LaPrune提供了一种数学上精确预算的可微top-k选择方法，在保持所选质量的同时控制掩码的集中度，且其硬度参数具有尺度不变性，优于固定温度的LapSum方法。

Abstract: Top-$k$ selection determines which components of a sparse model remain active. Hard selection blocks gradients, while continuous relaxations often couple mask hardness to the selected mass. We introduce LaPrune, a mathematically exact-budget differentiable layer that controls the normalized second moment while preserving the selected mass. A LapSum barrier preserves the selection mass, and a normalized second-moment constraint moves the mask from a dense equal-mass allocation toward hard top-$k$ at each budget. We derive a population prediction of the saturated fraction, a near-binary limiting law, and a tight worst-case guarantee on the near-zero fraction. The normalized hardness parameter is invariant to score scale, while a fixed LapSum temperature is not.

</details>


### [224] [SJEPA: Learning Elegant Latent Dynamics with Hybrid Symbolic-Neural Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04060)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SJEPA，一种无重建的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），通过混合符号-神经过渡模型学习具有紧凑符号描述的预测表示，并形式化了诱导动力学复杂度与表示坍塌之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）虽然能学习抽象状态，但其过渡模型通常是不可解释的神经网络黑箱。作者希望在不牺牲预测能力的前提下，学习到能够由紧凑符号规则描述的动力学表示，从而同时获得表示的可解释性和预测能力。

Method: SJEPA采用混合过渡模型：一个符号动力学定律加上一个正则化的神经校正项，用于处理所选语法之外的动力学。核心原则是学习"最简单的充分动力学"：通过表示约束保留信息丰富且不坍塌的预测坐标，并通过算子压缩机制偏好低复杂度的符号-神经过渡。框架支持交替式表示-方程学习和固定表示下的符号动力学拟合两种模式。

Result: 在受控摆钟实验中，联合学习相比事后拟合能发现明显更简单的符号动力学，且长时域 rollout 误差和散度更低；无约束的一步诊断验证了预测的坍塌捷径。在语法错配情况下，校正正则化能保留可表示的符号机制并将神经成分导向残差动力学。

Conclusion: 实验揭示了预测保真度、表示质量、符号简洁性和符号-神经分配之间存在可控的权衡。SJEPA证明联合学习符号动力学与表示可以同时提升表示的抽象性和预测的良好性，且需要谨慎约束以避免表示坍塌。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures learn abstract states by predicting target embeddings from context embeddings, but their transition models are typically opaque neural maps. We introduce SJEPA, a reconstruction-free JEPA framework that learns predictive representations whose induced dynamics admit compact symbolic descriptions. Its hybrid transition combines a symbolic law with a regularised neural correction for dynamics outside the selected grammar. The central principle is to learn the simplest adequate dynamics: representation constraints preserve informative, non-collapsed predictive coordinates, while operator compression favours low-complexity symbolic-neural transitions that remain predictively adequate. We formalise this principle through induced-dynamics complexity, analyse predictive-coordinate non-identifiability, and show that unconstrained operator compression creates a direct shortcut to representation collapse. The framework supports both alternating representation-equation learning and symbolic dynamics fitted to fixed representations. In controlled pendulum experiments, joint learning discovers substantially simpler symbolic dynamics with lower long-horizon rollout error and divergence than post-hoc fitting, while an unconstrained one-step diagnostic realises the predicted collapse shortcut. Under grammar misspecification, correction regularisation preserves the representable symbolic mechanism and directs the neural component towards residual dynamics. The results expose a controllable trade-off among predictive fidelity, representation quality, symbolic parsimony, and symbolic-neural allocation.

</details>


### [225] [Spend Bits Where Queries Look: KV Cache Vector Quantization with Attention-Preserving Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04074)
*Samuel Fernández-Menduiña,Amir Ziashahabi,Eduardo Pavez,Antonio Ortega,Salman Avestimehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NOVA-KV，一种基于变换编码的KV缓存量化方法。通过从校准统计量中推导闭式最优变换（键变换非正交），并利用注意感知失真与变换域MSE的广义Parseval关系，结合等体积分组的MSE最优矢量量化器，在每元素2比特下显著恢复长上下文检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM解码每一步都需要读取KV缓存，加载开销超过计算注意力本身，系统通量受限于带宽。减小缓存大小可同时提升解码速度和承载能力，但挑战在于：在不牺牲注意力乘积的前提下减少缓存，保持重构低成本，且固定每token比特数。现有2比特方法依赖正交变换，但要么数据无关，要么未从失真准则推导变换；且基于随机或Hadamard旋转仅均衡方差而非压缩能量，固定宽度标量量化在低码率下次优。

Method: 将KV缓存量化建模为变换编码问题，以注意力乘积误差作为失真度量。在高分辨率模型下，从校准统计量闭式推导键和值的最优变换。关键发现：最优键变换是非正交的，满足广义Parseval关系——注意感知失真等价于变换域的MSE，因此可直接对变换后键系数使用MSE最优矢量量化器。为满足固定宽度布局，将系数分组为等体积分区，使等大小码本在高分辨率模型下达到变速率最优。

Result: 在每元素2比特时，NOVA-KV相比标量量化方法以可比的吞吐量恢复大部分长上下文检索精度损失。

Conclusion: NOVA-KV通过将KV量化形式化为注意感知的变换编码问题，并利用非正交最优变换与MSE最优矢量量化的组合，在极低比特率（2比特/元素）下实现了优于现有标量量化方法的检索精度保持，且不牺牲解码吞吐。

Abstract: Long-context LLM decoding reads the key-value (KV) cache at every step. Loading it takes longer than computing attention over it, so throughput is bandwidth-bound. Hence, reducing the cache size can raise both decoding speed and serving capacity. The challenge is to reduce cache size while preserving the attention products, keeping reconstruction cheap, and using a fixed per-token bit count. At two bits per element, the most competitive methods rely on orthogonal transforms. However, existing techniques are either data-oblivious or use the query statistics without deriving the transform from a distortion criterion. Moreover, they rely on transforms built on top of random or Hadamard rotations, which equalize variances across entries rather than compacting energy, and fixed-width scalar quantizers, which are suboptimal at low rates. In this paper, we formulate KV cache quantization as a transform coding problem in which distortion is the error in the attention products. We derive closed-form optimal transforms for keys and values from calibration statistics, under a high-resolution model. We show that the optimal key transform is not orthogonal and satisfies a generalized Parseval relation: the attention-aware distortion becomes mean-squared error (MSE) in the transform domain. Thus, we can use MSE-optimal vector quantizers applied directly to the transformed key coefficients. To meet the fixed-width layout requirement, we show that grouping coefficients into equal-volume partitions makes equal-size codebooks attain the variable-rate optimum under the same high-resolution model. At two bits per element, our method, termed NOVA-KV, recovers most of the long-context retrieval accuracy lost by scalar quantization methods at comparable throughput.

</details>


### [226] [Spatiotemporal Graph Transformer for Traffic Intelligence in Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04075)
*Laha Ale,Letian Lin,Na Cao,Zheng Ma,Peng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时空图Transformer框架，用于边缘计算中的流量预测，通过图神经网络捕获空间相关性、Transformer自注意力学习长程时间依赖，并在真实蜂窝网络数据集上优于GCN-RNN、GCN-LSTM和GCN-GRU等基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算中流量预测对主动资源管理至关重要，但现有循环方法（如RNN、LSTM、GRU）只能捕获短期动态，难以在非平稳条件下建模长时间跨度的流量演化。需要一种能够同时建模空间相互作用和长时间时间依赖的新框架。

Method: 提出时空图Transformer框架：采用图神经网络（GNN）捕获服务区域间的空间相关性，利用Transformer自注意力机制从历史流量观测中学习长程时间模式，并将空间表示学习与时间推理解耦，实现大规模时空流量建模。

Result: 在真实蜂窝网络数据集上的大量实验表明，该图Transformer在多个预测时域上持续优于GCN-RNN、GCN-LSTM和GCN-GRU等循环图基线模型。生成的预测可实现更有效的主动资源预配置，相比被动管理策略降低了过载风险。

Conclusion: 图增强注意力机制在构建智能自适应的边缘计算系统中具有显著潜力，所提出的时空图Transformer为边缘计算中的主动资源管理提供了有效的预测工具。

Abstract: Accurate traffic forecasting is essential for proactive resource management in edge computing, where service demand evolves dynamically across both space and time. In practical cellular edge systems, traffic exhibits strong spatial correlations among neighboring service regions and long-range temporal dependencies driven by user mobility and application behavior. Existing recurrent forecasting approaches can capture short-term dynamics but often struggle to model long-horizon traffic evolution under non-stationary conditions. To address this challenge, we propose a spatiotemporal graph Transformer framework that jointly models spatial interactions and temporal dependencies for traffic forecasting in edge computing. The framework employs graph neural networks to capture spatial correlations among service regions and leverages Transformer-based self-attention to learn long-range temporal patterns from historical traffic observations. By decoupling spatial representation learning from temporal reasoning, the proposed approach provides an effective mechanism for large-scale spatiotemporal traffic modeling. Extensive experiments on a real-world cellular network dataset demonstrate that the proposed graph Transformer consistently outperforms recurrent graph-based baselines, including GCN-RNN, GCN-LSTM, and GCN-GRU models, across multiple forecasting horizons. The resulting forecasts enable more effective proactive resource provisioning and reduce overload risk compared with reactive management strategies. These results highlight the potential of graph-enhanced attention mechanisms for building intelligent and adaptive edge computing systems.

</details>


### [227] [SpecDrop: Parameter-Free Category-Conditioned Routing for Modular Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04084)
*Boyao Wang,Zhihan Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecDrop——一种无参数、无需学习的固定路由方案，用于检验Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）网络中性能瓶颈究竟是路由算法本身还是训练信号粒度与目标类别之间的对齐。在明确类别标签（如CIFAR-100、ImageNet-1K）的视觉任务中，SpecDrop超越等参数规模的基线；但当训练单元横跨多个类别（模糊分区，如语言建模、指令微调）时，其效果退化至与无路由基线无异。结论：粒度对齐而非算法选择决定了路由何时有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE网络依赖学习型路由器、门控和负载均衡损失来追求专业化，但在匹配总参数预算的条件下，学习型路由器可能不如等权重的无路由基线。作者想弄清楚根本瓶颈究竟是路由算法本身，还是训练信号粒度与目标类别之间的对齐关系。

Method: 提出SpecDrop：一种固定且无参数的路由方案。每个分支对其指定类别获得权重p_a，对其他类别获得小泄漏权重p_i>0，通过类别无关的固定分母合并，不学习任何路由参数，也不使用辅助损失，但推理时需要给定类别标签。在视觉任务（识别单超类标签）和模糊分区任务（语言建模、指令微调）上分别验证。

Result: 在CIFAR-100（ResNet-110）上达79.23%，ImageNet-1K（ViT-S/16）上达79.89%，分别超过参数匹配且不使用标签的基线（+4.75 over dense on CIFAR-100，+6.53 over No-Routing+SE on ImageNet-1K）。分支-类别对齐达58%/100%，屏蔽增益为0.00（CIFAR）/ +1.06（ImageNet）。在模糊分区任务中，路由机制的性能与无路由对照持平（在种子噪声范围内）。

Conclusion: 路由带来的增益本质上是类别监督的价值体现，而非路由算法的优越性。SpecDrop的贡献在于将类别标签转化为训练后的模块化结构，将输出空间限制在训练过程中内化。粒度对齐（而非算法选择）决定了路由何时真正有帮助——当训练单元与目标类别对齐时路由有效，模糊时则失效。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) networks pursue specialization through learned routers, gates, and load-balancing losses, yet at matched total-parameter budgets learned routers can underperform equal-weight No-Routing baselines. Is the bottleneck the routing algorithm, or the alignment between training-signal granularity and the target categories? We probe the question with SpecDrop, a fixed parameter-free routing scheme: each of $K$ branches receives weight $p_a$ for its assigned category and a small leakage $p_i > 0$ otherwise, merged through a category-independent fixed denominator, with no learned routing parameters and no auxiliary losses; the category label is required at inference. On vision tasks where each image has one superclass label (CIFAR-100 on ResNet-110; ImageNet-1K on ViT-S/16), SpecDrop reaches 79.23% on CIFAR-100 and 79.89% on ImageNet-1K, exceeding parameter-matched baselines that do not use the label (+4.75 over dense on CIFAR-100; +6.53 over the No-Routing+SE control on ImageNet-1K). These gains quantify what category supervision buys when deployed through routing -- not an advantage over label-aware deployments of the baselines: given the same label, masking a dense model's outputs is stronger for accuracy alone (85.2 / 83.7). SpecDrop's contribution is converting the label into trained-in modular structure: 58%/100% branch-category alignment, and masking gains of 0.00 (CIFAR) / +1.06 (ImageNet) -- the output-space restriction is largely internalized during training. On fuzzy partitions, where training units span multiple categories (SlimPajama-6B language modeling with a 30M Transformer; SuperNI instruction tuning over Llama-3.2-1B with LoRA), the routing mechanism reduces to the matched No-Routing controls within seed noise, the null our thesis predicts. Granularity alignment, not algorithm choice, localizes when routing helps. Code: https://github.com/Beryex/SpecDrop

</details>


### [228] [Out-Of-The-Loop Multi-Fidelity Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04113)
*Gustavo Sutter,Hao Wang,Luis Ricardez-Sandoval,Pascal Poupart,Agustinus Kristiadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多保真度贝叶斯优化（MF-BO）在实际场景中因最高保真度函数过于昂贵而无法纳入优化循环的问题，提出利用历史高保真度数据及任务描述符（显式给定或从非结构化元数据中提取）来弥补信息缺失，并在合成函数、化学和超参数优化等真实问题上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MF-BO假设最高保真度函数可以被查询且纳入优化循环，但在许多实际任务中这一假设不成立——最高保真度评估过于昂贵。而实践者通常拥有从先前实验中获得的、可能对当前任务有用的"黄金标准"高保真度数据，标准MF-BO算法无法有效利用这些历史数据。

Method: 作者首先证明标准MF-BO算法在上述真实场景下即使在理想假设下也是次优的；随后提出通过将历史高保真度数据与任务描述符（可显式给出或从非结构化元数据中提取）相结合来缓解该问题，从而将历史信息纳入优化过程。

Result: 在合成函数以及化学和超参数优化等真实世界问题上验证了所提方法的有效性，表明引入历史高保真度数据和任务描述符能够提升MF-BO的性能。

Conclusion: 标准MF-BO在最高保真度函数不可查询的场景下存在次优性，利用历史高保真度数据结合任务描述符可以有效地缓解这一问题，为实际应用提供了更实用的MF-BO框架。

Abstract: Black-box optimization is a ubiquitous problem in science and engineering, often dealing with expensive objective functions with cheaper lower-fidelity proxies available. Multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization (MF-BO) is a principled approach to this problem, leveraging correlations across different fidelities when querying the objective. However, for many important MF-BO tasks, the true highest-fidelity function is prohibitively expensive to be part of the optimization loop. Nevertheless, practitioners often have gold standard data (observations of the highest-fidelity function) obtained from previous experiments that might provide information for the current task. For instance, in molecular optimization, chemists often pick the top-$k$ candidate molecules using various computer simulations, and later reveal their true objective function values. In this work, we demonstrate the suboptimality of standard MF-BO algorithms in the real-world scenarios above, even under ideal assumptions. Next, we mitigate this problem by incorporating historical high-fidelity data accompanied by task descriptors---which can be explicitly given or extracted from unstructured metadata. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our methods on synthetic functions, as well as real-world problems in chemistry and hyperparameter optimization.

</details>


### [229] [LiNC: Lightweight Noise Correction via Per-Sample Trust and Gaussian Mixture Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04147)
*Abhishek Moturu,Babak Taati,Anna Goldenberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级标签噪声校正方法LiNC，通过对每个训练样本引入一个可学习的信任参数，在标准训练循环中动态决定是使用原始标签还是依赖模型自身预测，并利用高斯混合模型区分干净、模糊和噪声样本，实现软校正和硬校正两阶段的标签修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像数据集中普遍存在标签噪声（如标注者间差异、标注错误和模糊病例），这会严重损害在该数据上训练的机器学习模型的可靠性和临床有效性。现有方法往往需要复杂的架构修改或额外的计算开销，缺乏轻量且高效的解决方案。

Method: LiNC的核心思路是为每个训练样本引入一个可训练的信任参数（trust parameter），在训练过程中通过学习原始标签与模型自身预测分布的凸组合来决定何时采信标注、何时依赖模型。该方法利用梯度信号使干净样本与噪声样本的信任值在训练早期朝相反方向演变，形成可分分布，随后通过3分量高斯混合模型将样本分为干净、模糊和噪声三类，对噪声样本依次执行短时软校正和最终硬校正。

Result: 在MedMNISTv2的十个2D数据集上、最高50%标签噪声的条件下，LiNC在准确率和错误标签检测方面均取得了一致的性能提升。训练时间复杂度仍由基础网络主导，额外内存随训练集大小线性增长，渐近开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: LiNC通过仅增加每样本一个标量参数的极简设计，在保持训练效率的同时实现了有效的标签噪声校正和误标检测，为医学影像领域的机器学习模型提供了鲁棒且实用的标签噪声处理方法。

Abstract: Label noise is common in medical imaging datasets due to factors such as inter-rater variability, annotation errors, and ambiguous cases. This can severely undermine the reliability and clinical effectiveness of machine learning models trained using those datasets. To address this challenge, we introduce Lightweight Noise Correction (LiNC), which adds a single trainable trust parameter per training sample and learns when to use the observed label and when to defer to the model during a standard training loop. The key idea is to train using a convex combination of the observed label and the model's own predictive distribution, controlled by a per-sample trust parameter. We show that the gradient of this objective drives trust values in opposite directions for clean versus noisy samples in the early training phase, yielding separable trust distributions. We use a 3-component Gaussian Mixture Model over the trust values to separate them into clean, ambiguous, and noisy cases and then execute a short soft-correction phase on the noisy cases and a final hard correction phase. Experiments on ten 2D datasets from MedMNISTv2 under label noise of up to 50% show consistent gains in accuracy and strong mislabel detection. LiNC adds negligible asymptotic overhead: the training-time complexity remains dominated by the base network, with additional memory growing linearly with the size of the training set.

</details>


### [230] [MINT: Tensor Decomposition on Stacked Recurrence Matrices for Time Series Data Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04157)
*Kaamil Kaka,Audrey Der,Evangelos E. Papalexakis,Zachary Zimmerman,Vikram Jayaram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出张量化自相似矩阵作为一种新的时间序列数据挖掘原语，通过计算N×(n-m+1)×(n-m+1)规模的点图张量，并结合张量分解方法挖掘共聚类模式，在轨道交通、电力需求、风力发电机和汽车交通等多个领域验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归图作为一种时间序列数据挖掘原语已广泛应用于多个领域（如星光曲线、声音波形、CCT遥测），但现有方法在处理大规模时间序列数据集时存在扩展性和模式挖掘方面的局限。作者旨在提出一种新的张量化原语，能够自然地扩展到多变量数据集，并有效挖掘跨传感器共聚类模式。

Method: 提出MINT（张量化自相似矩阵）流水线：(1) 对N条长度为n的单变量时间序列计算点图（dot plots），构建大小为N×(n-m+1)×(n-m+1)的自相似性张量，其中m为子序列窗口长度；(2) 利用张量分解方法对所得张量进行挖掘，以发现共聚类模式。该方法基于张量结构天然支持多变量数据集的扩展。

Result: 在轨道交通、电力需求、风力发电机和汽车交通四个真实数据集上验证了MINT流水线的有效性。实验结果表明，该方法能够有效挖掘高度规则化数据集中以固定间隔出现的模体（motifs）所对应的跨传感器共聚类模式。

Conclusion: 张量化自相似矩阵（MINT）是一种有效且可扩展的时间序列数据挖掘原语，特别适用于包含规律性模体（其出现间隔具有周期性）的高度规则数据集，能够通过张量分解实现跨传感器共聚类模式的高效挖掘。

Abstract: Recurrence plots are a time series data mining primitive applied to a variety of domains (e.g. star light curves, sound waveforms, CCT telemetry). This work proposes tensorized self-similarity matrices as a primitive for univariate time series datasets ($N\times n$) of $N$ time series of length $n$ with a subsequence window of length $m$, and whose tensor-based nature is naturally extensible to multivariate datasets. The proposed method to compute this primitive computes dot plots of size $N \times (n-m+1) \times (n-m+ 1)$ from these datasets, where the subsequent tensor is mined using tensor decomposition methods to mine for co-clustered patterns. We demonstrate our results in mass rapid transit, electricity demand, wind turbine, and car traffic data, finding the MINT pipeline effectively co-clusters cross-sensor patterns in highly regular datasets containing motifs at regular intervals.

</details>


### [231] [Understanding Fault Tolerance of Adversarially Robust Pruned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04173)
*Manali Dangarikar,Cory Merkel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了剪枝、对抗训练和硬件故障注入三者之间的交互作用对卷积神经网络鲁棒性的影响，发现对抗训练虽提升了对输入扰动的鲁棒性，却增加了对硬件权重卡死故障的敏感性；而剪枝对故障敏感性影响不大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络部署在资源受限的神经形态硬件上，同时面临模型压缩（剪枝）、对抗扰动和硬件权重故障（如卡死零错误）三类挑战。已有研究通常单独考察这些因素，但它们对模型可靠性的联合影响尚未得到充分关注。

Method: 采用在MNIST上训练的三层紧凑CNN，设计了三个实验：(1)比较自然训练与对抗训练模型在同时存在硬件故障和对抗攻击下的容错能力；(2)评估剪枝对对抗鲁棒性的影响；(3)刻画故障率、对抗扰动幅度和剪枝水平共同作用的准确率联合曲面。

Result: 对抗训练能提升对输入扰动的鲁棒性，但增加了对卡死零权重故障的敏感性。与直觉相反，剪枝并未显著增加故障敏感性，不同剪枝水平在故障率和攻击强度变化下几乎不产生影响。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调在设计针对神经形态硬件部署的DNN时，需要联合考虑对抗鲁棒性与硬件可靠性，而非孤立地优化单一指标。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) deployed on resource-constrained neuromorphic hardware face three concurrent challenges: the need for model compression through pruning, vulnerability to adversarial input perturbations, and susceptibility to hardware-induced weight faults such as stuck-at-zero errors. While each of these factors has been studied in isolation, their combined effects on model reliability have received little attention. This paper presents an empirical investigation of how pruning, adversarial training, and hardware fault injection interact to affect the robustness of convolutional neural networks. Using a compact three-layer CNN trained on MNIST, we conduct three experiments: (1) comparing the fault tolerance of naturally and adversarially trained models under simultaneous hardware faults and adversarial attacks, (2) evaluating how pruning affects adversarial robustness, and (3) characterizing the joint accuracy surface across fault rates, adversarial perturbation magnitudes, and pruning levels. Our results show that adversarial training improves robustness against input perturbations but increases sensitivity to stuck-at-zero weight faults. Contrary to intuition, pruning did not significantly increase fault sensitivity, and varying the pruning level had little effect across fault rates and attack strengths. These results highlight the need to jointly consider adversarial robustness and hardware reliability.

</details>


### [232] [TS2TabPFN: Time Series Classification and Extrinsic Regression through Feature Extraction and a Tabular Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04174)
*Gabriel da Costa Merlin,Diego Furtado Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TS2TabPFN框架，将显式特征提取与TabPFN 2.5表格数据基础模型相结合，用于时间序列分类（TSC）和外生回归（TSER），在TSER任务上显著超越现有最佳模型，并在TSC上优于大多数最先进算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列方法存在范式割裂：基于特征的方法注重特征提取质量，而深度学习方法依赖复杂架构对原始数据的预测能力。这种割裂导致特征工程的可控性与端到端模型的自动化性能之间存在差距，缺乏将两者优势结合的方案。

Method: 提出TS2TabPFN框架，核心思路是将显式特征提取与TabPFN 2.5（表格数据基础模型）相结合：先对时间序列进行结构化特征提取，再将提取的特征输入TabPFN 2.5进行预测，从而同时利用特征工程的可解释性和基础模型的强大预测能力。

Result: 大量实验表明：TS2TabPFN在TSER任务上以统计显著性大幅超越最先进模型；在TSC任务上超越大多数当前最佳算法，具备鲁棒性和高效性。

Conclusion: 将基础模型与结构化特征结合能够克服单一范式的局限性，为时间序列任务建立了新的最先进水平（state-of-the-art）。

Abstract: Time series data are ubiquitous in practical applications, where classification (TSC) and extrinsic regression (TSER) have emerged as essential tasks for obtaining value from temporal sequences. While the literature has seen significant progress through feature-based and deep learning models, existing methods often focus either on the quality of feature extraction or on the intrinsic predictive power of complex architectures applied to raw data. This division creates a gap between the control offered by feature engineering and the automated performance of end-to-end models. This paper proposes TS2TabPFN, a framework that bridges this gap by integrating explicit feature extraction with TabPFN 2.5, a cutting-edge foundation model for tabular data, to leverage its predictive capabilities. Our extensive experimental evaluation demonstrates that TS2TabPFN significantly outperforms state-of-the-art models in TSER tasks with statistical significance, providing a robust and efficient alternative for TSC and surpassing most of the currently best-performing algorithms. These results suggest that combining foundation models with structured features overcomes single-paradigm limitations, establishing a new time series state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [233] [A Comparative Study of Feature Selection Methods for EHR Diagnosis Codes in Opioid Use Disorder Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04180)
*Zihan Ding,Yinan Liu,Tengfei Ma,Rachel Wong,George Leibowitz,Benjamin Littenberg,Xia Zheng,Richard N. Rosenthal,Fusheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对电子健康记录（EHR）驱动的阿片类药物使用障碍（OUD）预测，系统比较了五种特征选择范式（复发富集、基于NTK的早期梯度敏感性、LightGBM-SHAP、弹性网络和大语言模型引导的语义选择），在统一预处理与评估框架下考察下游预测性能、重采样稳定性和罕见诊断编码的代表性。结果表明特征预算增大可提升性能但边际收益递减，NTK敏感性在精度与稳定性间取得最佳平衡，LLM引导的选择虽单独性能较低但补充了临床上有意义的互补信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR预测模型中输入变量通常具有高维、稀疏、噪声大和冗余的特点，大特征集会增加计算负担和过拟合风险，并降低模型可解释性，从而限制了临床实用性。因此需要系统比较不同的特征选择方法，以在OUD预测中找到性能与可解释性之间的最佳平衡。

Method: 围绕诊断相关特征，在统一的数据预处理和评估框架下，比较五种特征选择范式：复发富集（recurrence enrichment）、NTK驱动的早期梯度敏感性（NTK-motivated early gradient sensitivity）、LightGBM-SHAP、弹性网络（Elastic Net）以及大语言模型（LLM）引导的语义选择。评估指标包括下游预测性能、重采样稳定性以及罕见诊断编码的表示能力。

Result: 预测性能随特征预算增大而改善，但超过中等规模后收益递减。NTK敏感性方法在预测精度和稳定性之间提供了最佳的整体平衡；LLM引导的特征选择虽然在单独性能上不如其他方法，但贡献了临床上有意义的互补信号。

Conclusion: 不同特征选择范式各有优劣，NTK敏感性是OUD预测中兼顾准确性与稳定性的优选方法，而LLM引导的语义选择可作为补充手段提供临床相关特征，提示多种特征选择方法的组合可能有助于提升EHR预测模型的实用价值。

Abstract: Feature selection is a critical step in electronic health record (EHR)-based predictive modeling, where input variables are often high-dimensional, sparse, noisy, and redundant. Large feature sets not only increase computational burden and overfitting risk, but also make model interpretation difficult, leading to limited usefulness in clinical settings. In this study, we focus on diagnosis-related features and compare five feature selection paradigms for opioid use disorder (OUD) prediction: recurrence enrichment, NTK-motivated early gradient sensitivity, LightGBM-SHAP, Elastic Net, and large language model (LLM)-guided semantic selection. We use a unified preprocessing and evaluation framework and assess each method by downstream predictive performance, resampling stability, and representation of infrequent diagnosis codes. Our results demonstrate that performance improves with larger feature budgets with diminishing returns beyond a moderate size. NTK sensitivity provides the best overall balance of accuracy and stability, and LLM-guided selection contributes complementary clinically meaningful signals despite lower standalone performance.

</details>


### [234] [Unscented KalmanNet: a hybrid deep learning filter with calibrated posterior covariance for nonlinear state estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04201)
*Minhyeok Ko,Abdollah Shafieezadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Unscented KalmanNet (UKN)，一种混合递归估计器，通过两个结构性不同的学习组件（NoiseNet和GainNet）增强UKF，同时保持其显式sigma-point协方差递归，在合成系统和真实飞行数据上均取得了优于现有滤波器的最佳状态估计精度和协方差校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的UKF在实际应用中因未知时变噪声统计和模型失配而退化，影响估计精度和协方差校准。现有的学习型滤波器大多基于EKF构建，要么放弃显式协方差的输出，要么学习不确定性但未纠正由失配引起的增益偏差。因此需要一种既能利用深度学习优势、又能保持UKF显式协方差递归结构的混合估计器。

Method: UKN在UKF框架基础上引入两个结构不同的学习组件：(1) NoiseNet预测时变的过程噪声和测量噪声协方差，作为对有界基线乘性修正，保证正定性；(2) GainNet对解析Kalman增益施加有界残差修正。训练采用校准感知的目标函数，结合状态误差、协方差一致性和创新一致性项，通过自适应权重联合优化精度与校准。

Result: 在三个合成系统和UZH-FPV真实飞行数据上，UKN取得全部四个示例中最低的聚合状态估计误差；合成案例中相比UKF的RMSE降低26.4%-49.7%；11组飞行序列留一交叉验证显示平均位置和速度RMSE分别降低22.4%和34.3%；折叠间变异最小，归一化NEES和实证覆盖率最接近标称值。

Conclusion: UKN通过将两个互补的学习组件嵌入UKF的sigma-point递推结构，在保持显式协方差输出的同时，同时解决了噪声统计未知导致的精度下降和模型失配导致的增益偏差问题，实现了精度与协方差校准的双重提升，优于UKF、KalmanNet和Bayesian KalmanNet等基线方法。
